  • Chapter 979: There Is No Way I Wouldn't Believe. (4)

The abbot, silently descending Mount Hua, glanced back.

At that terrifying gaze, Beop Gye unconsciously shrank back.

“... Mount Hua.”

This was not how he felt when he ascended this path. There was a strange discomfort, but he had no doubt that he would eventually get what he wanted.

To him, Mount Hua was like a wolf that only he could handle.

To ordinary people, a wolf was an object of fear, but to someone who could tame a wolf, it was nothing more than a dog with sharp teeth.

However, the dog he thought he had tamed was now baring its teeth. It was as if it hadn’t yet lost its wildness from when it roamed the fields.

“The Buddha showed mercy to everyone.”

“...”

“But he did not show mercy to one tribe. Do you know which tribe it was?”

“I don’t know.”

“The demons.”

Beop Gye closed his mouth, trying to say something. The abbot continued.

“That means Buddha also divided beings into those who could be saved and those who couldn’t. He was stricter than anyone else towards beings who couldn’t be saved and who only caused harm.”

The abbot glared at Mount Hua’s gate with a stern face.

“At least, I thought Mount Hua wasn’t the enemy, but it seems I was wrong.”

“... Abbot.”

The abbot bit his lip.

He looked at Beop Gye with slightly suspicious eyes as if he were contemplating something.

“... In the end...”

“Uh?”

“No.”

The abbot shook his head.

“First, the Yangtze River. We will handle that. Mount Hua comes next. But...”

And then he coldly rebuked.

“Someday, Mount Hua will also have to pay for this. Putting aside my personal feelings, they will definitely receive the price for turning a blind eye to something that would bring ruin to the world and for tarnishing the name of Shaolin.”

“That is what needs to be done, Abbot.”

Crack.

The prayer beads in the abbot’s hand shattered into pieces and fell.

The beads rolling out of the broken thread seemed to symbolize the current relationship between Shaolin and Mount Hua. What was once connected by a single thin thread was now severed.

“Mount Hua’s Sword Saint....”

The abbot gritted his teeth and stepped forward.

“This is your choice.”

After the abbot left, the main figures of Mount Hua gathered once more in the sect leader’s residence.

Among them, Hae Yeon sat proudly. Seeing his clear and bright eyes, everyone’s hearts naturally filled with pride.

“Everyone... thank you so much.”

“Don’t mention it, monk.”

“We’re not strangers, after all.”

“Don’t worry about it.”

A bright smile spread across Hae Yeon’s face, though he was slightly flustered.

He had left Shaolin, which had been like home to him. Yet, he didn’t feel sad because of these people. They were a different kind of family from Shaolin.

‘Right. This is where I should be...’

“You’re laughing?”

“...”

But.

Unfortunately, in that warm atmosphere, there was one person who didn’t smile.

A devil who seemed to have just emerged from hell glared at Hae Yeon with sharp eyes. A terrifying madness spread in his eyes. Hae Yeon was filled with fear as he shrank back.

“Are you laughing now?”

“T-taoist... I...”

“This brat is?”

As Chung Myung’s face grew redder, Hae Yeon slowly stepped back.

“Yah.”

“Uh?”

“Are you messing with me?”

“W-what do you...”

“Ehh!”

In the end, Chung Myung swiftly kicked Hae Yeon in the chest with both feet.

Kwang!

“Ackkk!”

Hae Yeon rolled into the corner after the sudden blow. As if his anger hadn’t subsided, Chung Myung ran toward Hae Yeon. Everyone was startled and began to pull Chung Myung away.

“Why, why are you doing that, Chung Myung!”

“Calm down now!”

“Someone bring us something sweet, fast!”

Once the word ‘sweet’ came out of the Five Sword’s mouth, it meant the situation was quite serious. The disciples of Mount Hua, who were terrified, joined forces with the Five Swords to suppress Chung Myung.

But Chung Myung continued to growl at Hae Yeon, scratching the ground like a blind dog.

“Let go! Will you not let go!? I am going to see that bald bastard’s hair growing on his head today!”

“What nonsense are you talking about!”

“No!”

Chung Myung shouted with flames in his eyes.

“If we had just kicked that bastard abbot out, you should have quietly stayed in the corner! Why are you following him and making new trouble! Uh?”

Flinch.

Hae Yeon lowered his head.

“This is so messed up! This is! Yah! Do you have any ill feelings towards Mount Hua or something? Are you saying that Shaolin and Mount Hua are fighting, and both of them are to die? This little brat was fed and sheltered for years, and now he is repaying us with kindness and enmity? Ah, no! Let me repay the kindness today! Ahh! Let go of me!”

“C-Calm down!”

Baek Cheon had a gut feeling that Chung Myung would really beat Hae Yeon until he was a mess if they let go of his hand, so they held on more desperately.

How ridiculous it must have been to say he was protecting Hae Yeon, but the person he was holding onto right now was one of the few people who could make that ridiculous statement not seem funny.

“What? Buddhism? Buddhism?! Yah, you bastard!”

“Ack!”

Unable to kick because he was held back, Chung Myung stretched out his leg with all his might and slapped Hae Yeon’s bald head with the sole of his foot.

“Even Buddha would have no answer if he saw you, and he would also break your head with a Bodhi tree branch! Are you really going to follow them? Them? Let me give you one hit; you will be caught by some nappers and laugh, chanting Amitabha!”

“No! Calm down now!”

“S-sect leader. Stop him! Sect leader...”

Yoon Jong, who turned his head to find Hyun Jong, flinched for a moment. Hyun Jong, mumbling something, had a face that looked almost half-dead.

“With Shaolin... of all the people, against Shaolin... I must be insane; why did I have to do that with Shaolin....”

“...”

Yoon Jong whispered to Baek Cheon in a hushed tone.

“Sasuk, the condition there seems to be worse?”

“Close your eyes quickly.”

“... Yes.”

After being mad for a while, Chung Myung’s muscles began to lose strength little by little, as if his anger had subsided.

“Anyway, nothing has worked out since I took that damned bastard in! What am I going to do about this? This bastard!”

Tears fell from Hae Yeon’s eyes.

Honestly, wouldn’t it be unfair from his perspective? He said he would just be excommunicated and told him not to get involved, but then this man just ran wild, kicked out the abbot, and now he was hitting Hae Yeon?

This was like forcibly dragging someone who was leisurely swimming out of the water, slapping him for being in the water in danger and taking his help away.

This was a robbery.

“Chung Myung, what is so bad about Shaolin?”

Chung Myung’s head suddenly turned. Baek Cheon, who saw that scary gaze, quickly turned his gaze away.

“You cannot say anything when I was fighting! Is Shaolin some kind of place for kids? It is bad! Of course, it is bad!”

“... it is just because you say so.”

“Eh. This man is pathetic.”

Chung Myung clenched his teeth.

‘Anyway, that guy’s temper.’

‘Poor monk Hae Yeon.’

‘He always blames others. Ughh.’

In fact, even if Hae Yeon’s case hadn’t happened, the relationship between Mount Hua and Shaolin had deteriorated to the point where it was impossible to mend the situation the moment the abbot kicked them out.

Hae Yeon’s case was nothing more than a wedge in that situation.

“Tsk.”

Chung Myung stopped there, aware of the situation. If it was his wisdom that led him to pause after understanding that much, he was indeed wise.

“Shaolin and... Shaolin. What should we do now? Now? Shaolin....”

Chung Myung’s anger might have subsided, but Hyun Jong’s spirit, which had departed, hadn’t returned.

Chung Myung softly coughed and began to comfort Hyun Jong.

“Ugh, calm down for now.”

“Calm down?”

“...”

“Do I look like I can calm down, you moron!”

At that moment, Hyun Jong rushed at Chung Myung with unprecedented speed and grabbed his ear.

“ACKKKK! Sect leader! Ear! Ear! My ear! It hurts!”

“This insane fool pulled his sword out on the abbot of Shaolin and ran? You made a big deal out of it while I was looking the other way, and what? Hae Yeon? Yah, you fool! Is this all because of monk Hae Yeon? It’s all your fault!”

“ACK! Ear! My ear! My ear is falling off! Ack!”

“Oh my, my ancestors...! What on earth did I do to deserve this guy...”

-Sorry.

“Uh?”

What was that sound just now...

“Ackkk!”

Chung Myung, who barely managed to escape, ran to the corner. Then, he protested with his neck all red.

“NO, what could be done about it! Those morons were going to cut off his head...”

“They weren’t cutting his head off! They were just sealing his blood points...”

“It would be better to have the head cut off. He’s just a fool without his martial arts! What good is a guy without martial arts? He’s worse than a street dog!”

“....”

What truly saddened Hae Yeon wasn’t Chung Myung’s words. As soon as he finished speaking, the other disciples nodded.

Tears spread in his tightly shut eyes.

‘Such bad people...’

He was moved for a moment, but he was a human. He was.

“Becoming enemies with Shaolin... the Shaolin... ancestors. I’ve ruined everything now. Now Mount Hua is bound to be ruined....”

“Don’t say such nonsense! A hundred years ago, we were almost at war with Shaolin, but we didn’t perish then, right?”

Hyun Jong looked at Chung Myung with a blank expression after those words.

“... A hundred years ago?”

“Yes!”

“A hundred years ago?”

“I am telling you, yes!”

“... Then we were ruined, right?”

“Uh?”

Oh?

... That was right, now that he heard it.

When Chung Myung’s words suddenly stopped, Hyun Jong sank into his place.

“What should I do... this is...”

“Ahem.”

Hyun Sang said after witnessing the entire exchange.

“If you were so worried about this, you could have resolved it nicely...”

“How can we resolve it nicely in that atmosphere! You moron!”

“How can you resolve it nicely with those Shaolin people themselves!”

Clearly, their directions were completely different. However, their actions weren’t much different.

Before long, even the sect leader had become influenced by Chung Myung. And it was a bad thing.

The faces of the Mount Hua disciples darkened in an instant.

“... Mount Hua is ruined now.”

“No, what ruin now! I am right here!”

“....”

Hyun Jong stared at Chung Myung and then let out a deep sigh.

“We are ruined.”

“No, this man is!”

“It is the sect leader, you moron!”

The moment Chung Myung glared, Baek Cheon ran over and kicked him off.

Chung Myung, who had rolled around in the corner, suddenly got up.

“No, I told you not to care about it! I told you those Shaolin ones are nothing special!”

“You told me before?”

“That they aren’t much of a great thing!”

“... just say two words with one mouth now, Chung Myung.”

How could he have so many different types of words coming out of one mouth? If someone saw Chung Myung, they would think a rainbow was coming out of his mouth.

Chung Myung was yelling tirelessly, while Hyun Jong was lost in thought, mumbling something to himself. Hae Yeon sat in a corner, looking depressed, and the Mount Hua disciples each voiced their own opinions.

Crack.

Hong Dae-Kwang, who had already taken a seat, couldn’t join in on the lively atmosphere. He just sat in a corner as if he didn’t exist, chewing on his rice cake.

“... This is a mess.”

But what could he say?

It was truly a Mount Hua-like scene.

“Anyway, that is it!”

Chung Myung said with an extremely confident tone.

“There is no need to worry about Shaolin right now. Those guys are probably too busy dealing with the Yangtze River right now. And there is no way we will be able to handle that. As long as the Four Precepts Alliance is alive and well, those people cannot do anything, right?”

Yoon Jong turned his head slightly and looked at Baek Cheon.

“What is he up to?”

“Hmm. To put it simply, it is like this.”

Baek Cheon smiled broadly and answered.

“I was angry and thoughtlessly yelled at him, but looking back, I think I made a mistake. I cannot say I did something wrong, so I am desperately trying to make this situation look good to others.”

“That is so concise, perfect.”

In times like these, the words of an authoritative figure definitely helped.

“No, it isn’t like that; it is the actual situation!”

“... right.”

“I am telling you! I am not making up stories!”

“Right... okay, we get it.”

“Woah, I am really going insane! Mister beggar! Say something!”

“Ahem!”

Hong Dae-Kwang, who had been chewing on a pancake in the corner, quickly gulped what was in his mouth and opened his mouth.

“Well... his intentions seem a bit impure, but well, what Mount Hua’s Sword Saint said, isn’t all wrong.”

“Uh?”

In the meantime, Hyun Jong, who had aged three years, looked back at Hong Dae-Kwang. Judging from the way the ears perked up, he must have felt that Hong Dae-Kwang’s words were worth listening to.

Chung Myung, who saw that scene, mumbled, ‘what I said is worse than a beggar now!’ but of course, those words didn’t get into Hyun Jong’s ears.

“You are not simply saying that, right?”

“Hahaha, Sect leader too.”

Hong Dae-Kwang smiled brightly and answered.

“Lies can only be told when the situation is right. Who would have imagined that the sect leader and Mount Hua’s Sword Saint would attack Shaolin’s abbot like that? I have never imagined this kind of situation in my life, so I cannot even think of making any stories up. Hehhe.”

“A-ahem!”

“Ehehehe!”

Hyun Jong and Chung Myung coughed at the same time.

The other disciples of Mount Hua nodded their heads as if they agreed with Hong Dae-Kwang’s words.

“It was a rare situation.”

“Ah. Such a rare one.”

“Isn’t this truly unprecedented then?”

“So noisy!”

“Such a mess!”

The older and youngest members of the sect were taking sides and persecuting the others, but Mount Hua didn’t find it odd at all.

“So, to conclude it...”

Hyun Jong asked Chung Myung in a heavy voice.

“Is it because of the current situation in the Yangtze River and the existence of the Four Precepts Alliance that Shaolin cannot do anything now?”

“Yes.”

Chung Myung shrugged.

“There is no need to speak of any excuses. If the situation was that easy, would that heavy-bottomed man have rushed all the way to Shaanxi?”

“... so him needing help wasn’t a lie?”

“Well, that is true. Anyway, even if he has bad feelings about it and all now, there is nothing we can do. He is probably busy trying to resolve the fight in the Yangtze River.”

Chung Myung chuckled.

“So we can just watch them fight and stop the war?”

“Hmm.”

Hyun Jong nodded with a heavy face.

It wasn’t a good thing that Shaolin turned into an enemy of Mount Hua, but it was fortunate that there was little chance of a problem occurring right now.

But after one worry was resolved, another concern, which had been put on hold for a while, popped up.

“Chung Myung.”

“Uh?”

“Is there a way to stop the war?”

“Hmmmm.”

Chung Myung looked at Hyun Jong with an odd gaze. Hyun Jong shook his head as if he knew what Chung Myung was thinking.

“I am not saying that we should step ahead. I am just asking this out of curiosity.”

“Yes. Well, then.”

Chung Myung seemed deep in thought for a moment before shaking his head.

“Depending on how Jang Ilso reacts, there will be a difference between an all-out war and a local war, but for now, there will be no way to stop the battle that will happen on the Yangtze River.”

“... Wouldn’t it be okay if the Namgung family retreated right now? Isn’t it possible for the abbot to persuade the Namgung family lord like you said?”

“Sect leader.”

“Uh?”

“Do you know why a crazy guy is called crazy?”

As Chung Myung snickered and tried to explain, Hyun Jong nodded his head while staring at Chung Myung.

“Hmm. Right.”

“Uh?”

“You mean you cannot communicate?”

“...”

“Yes, yes. I understand what it means.”

“... What do you know? I haven’t explained it properly yet?”

“Ahem. Well... I simply understood it.”

It was a good thing that he understood without having to explain it, but it felt strange that he stared at him throughout the process of understanding.

“Anyway... yes. It won’t make sense.”

Chung Myung nodded with a trembling face.

“If the abbot had enough virtue to control the Namgung family lord in the first place, this wouldn’t have happened.”

Jo Gul asked curiously.

“Is this happening because the abbot is lacking? Or is it happening because the Namgung family is too outright?”

“Hmm. That is a difficult question... isn’t it half and half? No, no. Let us say that the bald one is more at fault.”

He didn’t say that simply because he had any ill feelings towards the abbot.

He just imagined what would have happened if the person in charge of this situation had been Chung Mun and not the abbot.

It was obvious.

As expected, he would have dragged them over, saying, “Hehehe, family lord, come over here for a moment,” and then impressed them with beautiful admonitions and lofty conciliation.

Of course, the truth was hellish nagging and blatant threats that he would beat them up if they bothered him again, but in any case, he would have gotten what he wanted.

In the past, sahyung often resolved situations through gentle one-on-one talks whenever a problem arose. Of course, strangely, he would always have Chung Myung sit next to him when it happened.

He kept poking people who were about to die of boredom and making them irritated with...

Ah? Wait, this is...

“Anyway.”

Whether it was Namgung Hwang (Emperor) or Namgung Wang (King), he would be a crazy dog in front of the abbot, but they would be obedient puppies in front of Chung Mun. Everything in the world was relative.

So, from Chung Myung’s side, there was no way to hold the abbot accountable for the situation. Being incompetent wasn’t a crime, but being incompetent when you were in a responsible position was a crime.

“Then, eventually, war will break out.”

“Yes. There is a high chance of that.”

“Then, for now...”

Hyun Jong made a subtle expression.

“Even though the ties between us and Shaolin have deteriorated, wouldn’t it be better for the Nine Great Sects to seize the opportunity? If the Four Precepts Alliance gains the upper hand, the suffering of those living along the Yangtze River will be too great.”

“Hmm. But that is...”

“Uh? Were your thoughts different?”

“No, it isn’t that I have a different thought, but...”

Chung Myung scratched his cheek with his finger.

“The sect leader’s words aren’t wrong, but I don’t think that will happen.”

“... who do you think is leading the charge? Isn’t the Namgung family at the forefront? They’ve been honing their skills for the past three years and managed to defeat the pirates in one go. Don’t you think this momentum will last for a while?”

“If the Namgung family had stopped at the Northern bank, that might have been true. But those crazy guys are now on a ship heading to the island.”

“...”

“That means they want to fight on enemy territory. I know this because I’ve experienced everything from pre-warfare to water, air, and land battles...”

“... when have you been through all that?”

When Jo Gul tried to tease him, Baek Cheon responded, lightly rubbing his fingertips as if he found it odd.

“No, if you think about it, it isn’t wrong. That guy fought bandits on the mountain, pirates on the water, and faced Jang Ilso on the cliff by falling down.”

“... true.”

Sigh, these guys knew nothing.

A hundred years ago, what he was like.

He had been through everything: pre-warfare, water warfare, air warfare, ground warfare, and even underground warfare...! Uh?

“It’s harder than you think to fight those who’ve mastered water martial arts, right?”

“But when we fought on the Yangtze River before, it wasn’t really a problem for us, right?”

“Because they didn’t really want to fight us in the water.”

“Hmm.”

Hyun Jong made a face that seemed to show understanding.

But it was difficult for Chung Myung to explain this subtle difference in words. It wasn’t that important either.

“You’ll understand when you see it. Well, I don’t have any bad feelings towards the Namgung family either, so I hope they win if possible...”

Yoon Jong whispered softly to Baek Cheon.

“If someone who claims not to have ill feelings tried to kick the young lord’s crotch and make him a eunuch, how rotten must his character be?”

“... that’s just how he is. It’s Chung Myung.”

These people!

Chung Myung glared at them immediately.

“Ugh. Anyway... actually, the issue right now isn’t whether there will be a war.”

“Hmm? What are you talking about?”

“... the issue is the method of war. If the Namgung family simply loses, there won’t be a problem...”

Chung Myung’s expression turned strange.

“If not, the problem will get serious.”

“....”

“No, maybe...”

Chung Myung’s lips curled up.

“It might have already started. The Black Dragon King wouldn’t be the kind of person who would be so easygoing.”

The Yangtze River was covered in deep darkness.

“Hmm.”

A man stood on the riverbank and stared into the middle of the river. It was clearly in the middle of the wide river, but it was brightly lit as if large flower baskets were floating in a row.

The place that the people called Plum Blossom Island.

The Namgung family, who occupied Plum Blossom Island, kept the night as bright as day in preparation for a night attack.

“Such an idiot.”

A large man stood on the other side of the riverbank.

The Black Dragon King looked at Plum Blossom Island with a big smile.

“Namgung Hwang, the Emperor’s Sword. He might have skills, but he is a terribly simple-minded person and a moron to lead a family. How dare he confront me on this Yangtze River.”

Even he had no way of dealing with the Evil Sect in the Yangtze River war which happened in the past. At that time, it wasn’t just the Namgung family he had to contend with.

If the Namgung family, Shaolin, Wudang, Qingcheng, and even Mount Hua with Green Forest were to come, it would be impossible to stop them, not just for the Black Dragon King, but even for the real Dragon King.

However, the only one who invaded this Yangtze River now was just the Namgung family.

“It seems that the experience back then has instilled arrogance in them.”

It was a pleasant experience, but on the other hand, it was no different from looking down on them, so it felt bad.

However, this dirty feeling would become even more pleasant when the Yangtze River turned red with the blood of those Justice faction people.

“We should show them how terrifying the river can be. Let us get started.”

“Yes! Black Dragon King!”

As soon as the Black Dragon King finished speaking, a group of people emerged from the shadows behind him.

“Make it clear to them what it feels like to have your feet not touching the ground.”

Upon hearing these words, the pirates plunged into the river without responding.

“Morons,”

The Black Dragon King stroked his long beard.

“If the Namgung family is like that, we will end up winning... well, Alliance Lord, you will have to pay a big price, right?”

His sinister laughter spread softly through the still waters of the Yangtze River.

“There is nothing wrong with the guards!”

“Hmm.”

Namgung Myung nodded his head repeatedly. Namgung Do-Wei, who was following him as if guarding, observed the condition of those on duty with sharp eyes.

“No matter how excessive the guarding is, it isn’t too much. Do your best not to miss even the smallest thing.”

“Yes!”

Namgung Myung nodded lightly and moved to the next area with Namgung Do-Wei.

“What do you think, young lord?”

“Yes, uncle.”

Namgung Do-Wei looked back at Plum Blossom Island with renewed eyes and said.

“It is fortunate that the island isn’t that big. If it remains like this, even if the pirates do invade, it will not be difficult to defend.”

“I was thinking the same.”

The people guarding Plum Blossom Island were not just the Blue Sky Swordsmen. Other armed forces, following the orders of Namgung Hwang, had also joined in. Even if they couldn’t, eight-tenths of the Namgung family’s forces were gathered on Plum Blossom Island.

With that much strength, it would be more than enough to fight against the pirates of the Yangtze River.

“The fact that the island is narrow will actually be an advantage for us.”

“It would be difficult for the pirates, who rely on numbers rather than the skills of warriors, to demonstrate their strength here, right?”

“Yes.”

Namgung Myung smiled, pleased with the answer.

His nephew, who would later become the head of the Namgung family, was truly worthy of being called a genius. He wasn’t just excellent in martial arts but also had an eye for reading situations.

“You learned well from my brother.”

“Yes, uncle.”

“To become a great warrior, it is enough to work hard and diligently. However, that alone isn’t enough for someone to lead the family; you have to be more daring and know how to foresee a situation.”

“I always think about it.”

“Nice.”

Namgung Myung nodded at that.

‘I should thank Mount Hua’s Sword Saint for this.’

In the past, Namgung Do-Wei often showed arrogance, which worried Namgung Myung. It was inevitable because of the Namgung family’s nature and the influence of his father, but being confident and arrogant was never the same.

Fortunately, after losing to Mount Hua’s Sword Saint, he realized his own shortcomings. He also transformed into a completely different person after experiencing the Yangtze River War.

If he continued to gain experience like this, he might become the head of the family and surpass Namgung Hwang.

‘It won’t be easy, though.’

People like Namgung Hwang cannot be made by teaching them skills. They must have a natural talent for it.

“Do you know the Lord has decided to occupy this land?”

“... I am not sure about that part, uncle.”

Namgung Do-Wei looked around with a slightly trembling face. The dark, dark river conveyed a sense of anxiety to him.

“It is true that we have an unforgivable grudge with the pirates. But, I do not understand why we must confront the pirates alone. Didn’t the Namgung family clearly inform the world of our intentions?”

“You are right.”

Namgung Myung quietly nodded.

“If we occupy this Plum Blossom Island, we will eventually become a sticking-out awl. If we hesitate, we might end up facing not just the pirates but also the Four Precepts Alliance alone.”

“I think the same. No matter how much I think about it, the loss outweighs the gains.”

“Yes. That is the right thought. But, that is the thought of ordinary people like us.”

“... Uh?”

Namgung Myung grinned.

“The Lord must be thinking the same thing. Nevertheless, there is another reason why the Lord chose this difficult path.”

“Can I ask what that is?”

“If we back down here, we will end up just being another sect who follows Shaolin’s orders.”

Namgung Do-Wei’s face hardened at the unexpected answer.

“The Five Great Families. It is really a fancy name. But the people of the world think of the Five Great Families as something that belongs to the Nine Great Sects.”

“Uncle, that is a...”

“That is the reality. When the pirates took control of the situation, did the people of the Nine Great Sects think of us? Or did they think of Shaolin and the Nine Great Sects?”

Namgung Do-wei couldn’t answer. No, there was no need to answer.

“That is why the Lord took a risk. The current situation of the Five Great Families isn’t so good. It is because the Tang family, which formed the backbone, has distanced itself from us.”

“... Yes.”

“What would happen if we acted according to the will of Shaolin at a time like this? We would probably never escape the name of the Nine Great Sects.”

Only then did Namgung Do-Wei nod.

Thinking about the situation like this, it felt good.

The news that the other Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families responded lukewarmly to the call of Shaolin had reached the Yangtze River. Now, the influence of Shaolin and the Five Great Families was lower than ever. So, if not now, when else would they attempt something like this?

“The Lord wants the Five Great Families and the Namgung family to be famous, not the Nine Great Sects and Shaolin. He is willing to take some risks for that.”

“But that is not all.”

Namgung Myung, who was urging his feet, stopped in his tracks and looked back at Namgung Do-Wei. Namgung Do-Wei spoke with a confident face.

“There must be that reason, but Father is not the type of person to do something for that reason alone. I believe that there is a spirit of harmony within him.”

A pleased smile formed on Namgung Myung’s lips.

“You are saying the obvious thing. Of course, that is the first thing.”

“If we don’t do what we shouldn’t do and continue to do what we should do, won’t fame naturally follow? Like... Mount Hua.”

Namgung Myung nodded.

“Yes. That is how it should be.”

Mount Hua.

Just a few years ago, it was a name that had vanished from memory. However, now Mount Hua’s fame surpassed even the Namgung family, who were called the head of the Five Great Families.

With determination, not strength, Mount Hua had achieved what the Namgung family so desperately wanted.

“They are amazing.”

“Yes, they are amazing, but we can do it too.”

Namgung Myung patted Namgung Do-Wei on the shoulder. This youthful spirit sometimes warmed even his cold blood.

“Yes. It can be done...”

Namgung Myung’s gaze suddenly turned towards the river as he smiled and tried to hurry his steps. At that serious expression, Namgung Do-Wei also hardened his face and followed him.

But all Namgung Do-Wei could see were the calm waves.

“... uncle?”

“Hmm?”

Namgung Myung’s sword was pulled out instantly. The pure white sword qi, quickly emitted, roughly cut through the water, and went into the river.

Paat!

A splash of water arose. And...

Where the ripples had subsided, something large had floated up.

Namgung Myung’s face relaxed only after confirming the identity.

“... is that a carp?”

“It seems like a human. It is understandable that uncle can make a mistake.”

“Right.”

Namgung Myung shook his head. The blood the carp shed had spread across and shone on the river.

“We wasted our time. We don’t have enough time to check on everyone and stay in position. Let us go.”

“Yes, uncle.”

Namgung Myung, who led Namgung Do-Wei, walked ahead and glanced back at the water.

‘This seems ominous.’

The water, whose depth was unknown, kept frustrating him.

“This won’t be a place to stay for long.”

He hurried his steps, shaking his head.

At that moment.

One by one, things that looked like fish heads or small balls appeared on the surface of the Yangtze River, which was covered in darkness.

Anyone with good eyesight, who could see through the darkness, would be able to tell that they were the heads of people wearing specially made clothing.

“Captain.”

“Where are the enemy ships?”

“There are a total of five. They are all docked on the southern side.”

“Five, huh...”

The one called Captain nodded with a stern face.

“And wounds?”

“Not much. But... the Namgung family is the Namgung family. Who would have thought he would notice that?”

One of the members looked down at his shoulder with a stern face. The skin was ripped open, and the bleeding wouldn’t stop. If they hadn’t used their wits to cut down the carp passing by, wouldn’t the whole thing have been in vain?

“The opponent is the Namgung family. You cannot let your guard down, even for a moment.”

“Yes, Captain.”

“We will finish this in a jiffy.”

“Yes!”

When he gestured with a few instructions, the others nodded as if they understood and dived deep again. The Captain, who confirmed that everyone had started diving, took a look at Plum Blossom Island with both eyes and followed them down into the water.

Deeper and deeper.

They dove endlessly into the dark river with almost no visibility, moving while touching the bottom. No matter how good their senses were, they wouldn’t be able to detect the movements of these people moving along the riverbed.

Because there were countless fish larger than them swimming above their heads.

But that didn’t mean they could be completely at ease.

The closer they got to the island, the shallower the water became, increasing the chance of their presence being detected. This was the moment when they absolutely must not let their guard down.

They all looked up as they crawled along the riverbed.

Usually, docks were built where the river met the land, where the water was deepest and the slope was steepest.

What they saw when they raised their heads was the bottom of a large ship. Its fragile insides were exposed without much care.

‘They are not that stupid.’

A few Namgung family warriors were swimming around the ship, guarding it. This meant that they also understood how important the ship was.

But darkness was thicker underwater than above. No matter how alert they were, they wouldn’t be able to find their tracks.

The Captain laughed at the Namgung family’s warriors, who were completely unaware of their presence beneath their feet.

‘Namgung family?’

Of course, they were an incredibly strong and dangerous opponent.

But then, this was the middle of the Yangtze River.

They should have understood what it meant to have an island in the middle of the Yangtze River.

‘Now is the time to pay the price.’

The Captain gestured to the ship above.

Then, all his men nodded and began to surface at once. The bubbles created by their rapid movements rose together like crackers.

The harpoons in their hands each took on a bright blue qi.

Kwaaak!

Soon, the sharp harpoons stretched out at once, and the sharp blades cut through the water countless times.

Kwaang!

And with a loud bang, they all crashed into the bottom of the ship.

The bottom of the ship, made of thick wood, twisted and broke, and the black water poured into the ship in an instant.

“W-what!”

“An attack! Damn it! A night raid!”

The Namgung family warriors, who saw the ship tilting after the bang, shouted with pale faces.

“S-Stop them! We need to protect the ship!”

Screams, shouts, and confusion colored the night of Plum Blossom Island.

The fuse that had been out for a long time was finally rekindled.

Ships didn’t sink so easily, shocking as it might be.

There was a space under the floor of a ship. So even if there was a hole in the floor and it tilted and twisted, it usually took a considerable amount of time for the entire ship to sink.

That was common sense for normal people.

But.

Rumble!

That common sense was now being shattered before their eyes.

Where on earth did they attack, and how did they attack? That huge ship was being sucked into the river in an instant.

The sight of the water dragging the ship as if gulping it was enough to instill fear in the onlookers.

“The s-ship is...”

If someone had attacked them, they wouldn’t have been so shocked. However, to them, who were not used to water battles, it was an absurd shock that the ship was attacked before the person was.

The moment someone came to their senses, the second ship tilted to the side with a loud noise.

“S-Stop it! Damn it! Stop that quickly!”

A voice, more like a scream than a shout, rang out. Those who barely came to their senses at the sound looked at the ship.

Right. They had to stop it. If they left it as such, all the ships would sink.

But how to do that?

How could they stop an attack that came unseen and sank the ship?

“Jump in! The pirates are attacking from inside the water!”

There were always those who were quick to judge. They often showed the way to those who were slow to think. Those who heard the clear answer rushed into the water as if there was nothing to think about.

Those who flew in with footwork crashed into the water like shells, and huge sprays of water rose continuously.

If there had been someone who could leisurely watch the spectacle, they might have applauded, saying it was a great sight. But unfortunately, not a single person had the luxury of doing so.

Rumble!

At that moment, another ship tilted with a loud noise and was sucked down, creating large bubbles.

“Those bastardddsss!”

Yeop Sang of the Blue Sky Swordsmen shouted and threw himself into the water.

Splash!

Yeop Sang’s body, having leaped into the water, felt a tremendous shock. The faster he jumped, the greater the impact of passing through.

There was something else that truly shocked him.

The fact that he couldn’t see anything in front of his eyes.

Darkness in Heaven and Earth.

The river on this dark night was darker than he had imagined.

The darkness was so thick that even his eyes, trained in martial arts, couldn’t see an inch ahead. The moment he encountered it, a shiver ran down his spine. It was a fear that was not easy to overcome.

But, fortunately, or unfortunately, Yeop Sang wasn’t given time to deeply indulge in the fear.

Puak!

In the face of the eerie sensation of something piercing his chest and lower abdomen, fear was nothing more than a luxury.

Yeop Sang’s mouth opened. Dark water poured in ceaselessly through the open mouth, filling his stomach and lungs.

“Groupp.”

Air bubbles emerged from his gaping mouth. His eyes, barely accustomed to the darkness, confirmed that the long object piercing his chest and stomach was the harpoon of the pirates.

“Gulp.”

But that was all.

The vision that had barely been able to distinguish the outline was suddenly filled with darkness again.

A darkness so deep and profound that it was death.

Yeop Sang’s body, which had stopped moving, slowly rose to the surface as if floating. Not only Yeop Sang but also those who had hastily jumped into the water, rose up simultaneously as several bodies.

If it wasn’t for the water... no, even if it were water, if it weren’t for this time of the night, the result would never have come about.

But the river, shrouded in darkness, was harsher than they could have imagined.

In a place where you could neither see, feel, nor breathe, everything they had learned became meaningless. Those who failed to choose a battlefield advantageous to them in their urgency paid a heavy price.

“Soooo! Namgung Sooo!”

“AKKKK!”

The Namgung family swordsmen screamed at the top of their lungs as they were witnessing corpses rising from under the water.

And then.

Rumble!

As if to snatch away all hope, another ship began to sink. The blood drained from the faces of the Namgung family swordsmen.

‘Again...’

Now, only one ship remained floating properly on the surging waters.

If they lost that one, they would have no way off the island.

They felt the urgency to act, but their feet wouldn’t move. Those who had jumped into the water quickly rose as corpses, so who would dare to jump in?

“Get out of my way! I am coming!”

At that moment, an angry voice was heard from behind, and one man leaped towards the water without a moment’s hesitation.

“Euk!”

“Y-young lord!”

“It is dangerous!”

The Namgung family swordsmen shouted in shock as they saw Namgung Do-Wei jumping into the river. But he had already plunged in like a hawk targeting fish in the river.

Namgung Do-Wei disappeared in an instant with a rising splash of water.

As the young lord, the heir to the family risked his life by jumping into the water, the eyes of the swordsmen who witnessed him burned with determination.

“Protect the young lord!”

“We must protect the young lord, both the ship and the lord!”

“There is no fear in Namgung’s name! Let us go!”

The swordsmen guarding the riverbank and the swordsmen of Blue Sky, who arrived late, all followed Namgung Do-Wei with newfound courage, ashamed of their earlier hesitation.

Until they heard a desperate scream behind them.

“NOT THEREEEE!”

Those who had rushed into the water looked back in surprise.

Namgung Myung, who came running, was screaming loudly, his face clearly pale.

“N-Not the ship! Port! Protect the port point! We have to protect the port!”

“Uh?”

“Port? Isn’t it called a port for a reason? Before the ship, the port is...”

Kwaaang!

At that moment, a huge plume of water shot up with a massive explosion, engulfing the long port built along the river. Unable to withstand the force, the wood shattered into pieces and bounced onto the river like firecrackers.

Kwaang!

“Ahh...”

Namgung Myung’s face turned even paler as if he had seen a ghost.

He could clearly see the towering water column swallowing the White Thunder Cannons that had been placed on the port.

“No... No...”

He collapsed on the spot, his legs giving out at the sight.

Kwang!

At the same time, the last ship they had tried to protect began to sink under the water with a loud noise.

Namgung Myung stared blankly at the scene. His face looked as if he had lost his mind.

The bizarrely long dock and the White Thunder Cannons installed there.

These two were the keys to defending Plum Blossom Island. The dock was their lifeline, providing them with a place to stand and swing their swords, allowing them to narrow the distance to the land and survive.

The White Thunder Cannons were the only line of defense that completely blocked the ships approaching the port, their lifeline.

But now, they had lost both the land they could fight on and the weapons that could block the approach of ships. Even the ships that would become their feet were gone.

‘W-wait, the weapons?’

Namgung Myung hurriedly turned his head.

As expected, he saw the Namgung Family warriors rushing in at once after hearing the commotion.

“D-Don’t come!”

Namgung Myung screamed until his throat turned hoarse.

“Hold your ground, you morons! You must guard the White Thunder Cannons deployed around the island! Go back! Go back right now!”

The shout was desperate.

But it was too late. When the warriors heard the voice and hurriedly turned back, they encountered a completely destroyed cannon and a white lightning cannon that had vanished as if it had never existed.

“T-this is...”

The same scene was found everywhere else.

The pirates waited for the commotion, and once the warriors ran towards it, they leaped into the river, destroyed the cannons with ease, and escaped.

Darkness and water.

Those who could manipulate these two elements at will were the opponents of the Namgung family, renowned for their swordsmanship. What could they do with swords when they couldn’t even face their adversaries?

Those who returned to Namgung Myung with heavy hearts prostrated themselves on the spot.

“L-leader. The port is destroyed.”

“We failed to protect it.”

“....”

Splash. Splash.

Namgung Do-Wei, emerging from the water, clenched his teeth in frustration as he surveyed the island’s dire situation.

“... we lost them.”

Namgung Myung looked around the island with bewildered eyes, overwhelmed by the series of bad news.

He had lost everything he should never have lost: the port, the ship, and the White Thunder Cannons.

This place could no longer be called Plum Blossom Island.

Plum Blossom Island, stripped of its weapon against the enemy and the port that had restricted enemy movements, was now nothing more than a large dock floating in the middle of the river. The Namgung family had become like rats caught in a trap.

They were beaten. They were smashed without hope.

Did they let their guard down? No.

Did they underestimate the pirates? That wasn’t the case either.

The reason they were beaten so thoroughly was that they failed to understand that this was an island in the middle of the river. No matter how ferocious the lions were, they never realized they would become food for crocodiles under the cover of darkness.

“We were badly beaten.”

Namgung Myung clenched his teeth at the loud voice coming from behind him.

“L-Lord!”

His eyes trembled as he turned around.

Namgung Hwang, who had been walking briskly toward them, threw something he was holding in his hand.

Three heads.

The heads, clearly those of the pirates, rolled on the ground.

“Fucking flies.”

Namgung Myung knelt down and lowered his head close to touching the ground.

“Lord! I have disobeyed orders and made a mistake. Please punish me!”

“Moron!”

Namgung Hwang shouted at the sight.

“Raise your head! The Namgung family never bows down their heads carelessly!”

“L-Lord...”

“What is the problem? The ship? A cannon? A port? What is the problem with losing something like that?”

“....”

“Even if we lose our feet, they will not be able to enter this island! We won’t allow even a single pirate to step foot on this land as long as the Emperor’s Sword, Namgung Hwang, protects it!”

Namgung Hwang violently drew his sword and shouted.

“It doesn’t matter if it is a pirate or the Black Dragon King himself! We just have to make it clear that those who wish to show off their tricks can never stand a chance against the sword of the Namgung family! Do you understand?”

“Yes!”

Namgung family swordsmen responded with newfound courage at those words.

Namgung Hwang, who had made sure to regain the lost morale, glanced at the dark river with a firm face.

‘... this isn’t looking good.’

He was the one who caught the morale which was falling, but he didn’t know that the problem would be this serious.

‘Damn it.’

The situation began to sway in a direction he wasn’t pleased with.

Like a ship being sucked into the vortex that had formed in the middle of the river.

It was a very long night.

During the terrifying night, which they had never experienced before in their lives, the Namgung family’s swordsmen had to endure the fear that the pirates might invade at any moment.

But they couldn’t attempt to escape the island. Hadn’t they already experienced firsthand how dangerous the river was when darkness fell?

In the end, the Namgung family swordsmen had to stay up all night with their eyes wide open.

And when dawn finally broke, they faced despair deeper than the darkness.

Namgung Myung groaned.

“... the battleship of the pirates.”

“Hmm.”

Namgung Hwang frowned.

The moment the sun rose from the east and illuminated the river, the ships surrounding the island in the middle of the river revealed themselves. They were all armed with large cannons.

“...that fucking loach.”

Namgung Hwang gritted his teeth.

At a glance, it seemed as if he had gathered as many pirates from the Yangtze River as he could. It was a sight that showed their determination to not let even a single ant escape.

“Blue Sky leader.”

“Yes, Lord!”

“How do we break through?”

Namgung Myung bit his lips tightly.

“... it seems difficult, my lord.”

“And the reason?”

He gulped dryly and answered.

“The distance to the riverside is too far. It isn’t a distance that you cannot cross if you try hard... but it is impossible to reach the riverside with the cannons blocking the approach and arrows raining down.”

Namgung Hwang bit his lip.

“What if I cross the river alone? I don’t know about the members of the Blue Sky troops, but it wouldn’t be that difficult for me and the elders to leave.”

“... it would be possible. But...”

Namgung Myung hesitated, unable to say what was on his mind.

“Even if the Lord and the elders break through the encirclement and reach land... the problem is that it wouldn’t make a difference.”

This was an island.

A ship was needed to transport people to land. Even if Namgung Hwang went to the shore to find a ship and returned to the island, how could he deal with all those ships around, alone?

In the end, the ship he found would sink under the barrage of shells.

“But wouldn’t it be better than now?”

“Lord...”

Namgung Myung’s lips turned pale as he bit down on them. It was embarrassing to say this, but he had no other choice.

“... the moment the Lord and the elders leave this island, the pirates will attack without waiting for you. If that happens...”

“Um...”

Namgung Hwang understood the latter part without needing to hear it and sighed. Namgung Myung spoke in a gloomy voice.

“Those who remain on the island...”

“They will be annihilated.”

Namgung Hwang mumbled softly and closed his eyes.

In the sect, an absolute warrior was responsible for more than half of the sect’s entire strength. The Namgung Family, without Namgung Hwang, could never match the pirates.

The reason was quite simple.

Amidst the ships surrounding the island, a black ship stood out the most.

‘Black Dragon Ship...’

The Black Dragon King must be on that.

There was no need to bother with the elders. The moment Namgung Hwang stepped aside, the Black Dragon King would sail his ship to the island and attempt to land. Who in the Namgung family would face the Black Dragon King without Namgung Hwang?

On this island with nowhere to run, the Black Dragon King would be no different from a cat jumping into a swarm of rats in the poison.

“In the end... there is nothing we can do.”

“Lord...”

Namgung Hwang’s face was calm. He didn’t seem that angry.

But Namgung Myung and Namgung Do-Wei clearly saw it: blood flowing down his clenched fists.

He was holding back the anger, clenching his fists so hard that his nails were digging into his palms. He never showed such a look to the other Namgung family members.

“Uh!”

Namgung Hwang snorted briefly and growled.

“They wouldn’t dare invade for now.”

Soon, his booming voice spread loudly.

“Anyway, those fools won’t be able to approach the island. If that is the case, the standoff will only continue. Don’t waste your energy guarding the position uselessly. Everyone, go and rest. There are enough pavilions, so there should be no problem with you resting.”

When the Namgung family swordsmen looked at him with anxious eyes, Namgung Hwang spoke bluntly again.

“Don’t worry. If we just hold out on the island for a while, everything will be resolved!”

“Lord....”

“Blue Sky Leader!”

“Yes, lord!”

“Organize the crew into groups and have them rest in turns. Be on guard, but don’t let your guard down! Just be on guard when the ships approach the island.”

“Yes!”

Namgung Myung quickly formed groups and called the members. Then, he semi-forcibly pushed the others into the pavilion.

The anxious members looked uneasy about going inside the pavilion where they couldn’t see the outside to rest. Still, it was true that there was no other way, even if they were guarding the outside, so they eventually followed Namgung Myung’s instructions.

When the situation was somewhat resolved, Namgung Hwang quietly placed Namgung Myung and Namgung Do-Wei in separate places. He moved to a place where the other family members couldn’t see him and asked with two different expressions than before.

“What do you think we should do from now?”

He wasn’t stupid either.

There was no way he wouldn’t understand that the situation was going extremely wrong.

“Lord...”

Namgung Myung opened his mouth with a face full of humiliation.

“Right now... there is no way out.”

“... No way out?”

“Yes, lord... first, we have to protect this island. If we try to lift the siege carelessly, Namgung will be finished that day.”

“...”

“What the Lord said is right. No matter how skilled the pirates are, they will not dare land on the island that the Namgung family is guarding.”

“... It’s not that I can’t think of anything, but I don’t believe I need to.”

Namgung Myung, struck by the point, remained silent.

Even if a tiger was caught in a trap, it would be suicidal to approach it while it still has strength. An experienced hunter would wait patiently for the tiger to weaken.

When the tiger lost the strength to resist, the hunter would approach and cut its throat without damaging the body.

“Alright. Let’s say that’s all we can do for now. So, if we hold out, will the situation change?”

“... Reinforcements might come.”

“Reinforcements?”

Namgung Myung nodded weakly.

“Yes... there’s no way the Shaolin or the Nine Great Sects would allow our Namgung family to be surrounded by pirates. If they come to our aid, we can lift the siege and escape the island.”

Namgung Hwang’s face twisted.

“Haven’t you already experienced their strength?”

“L-Lord,”

“We couldn’t even properly fight back against their attacks. Don’t you know that the reason for the one-sided victory three years ago wasn’t because they were stronger than us, but because the Hegemony Lord lured us into the Black Dragon Pirates?”

“....”

“But do you think Shaolin can face them on the river now?”

“Lord...”

It wasn’t a voice filled with anger or sternness.

Rather, it was a question posed with such indifference that it was almost chilling.

“Even so, it is Shaolin.”

“....”

“They might find it difficult to completely save us with their strength, but they can at least find a way out.”

“... Right.”

Namgung Hwang nodded and looked up.

His shoulders seemed a little smaller than usual.

“Was it greed?”

“...L-lord.”

“I started this to escape the shadow of Shaolin and stand completely on Namgung’s name, but instead, I ended up being captured by the pirates and waiting for Shaolin’s help. I cannot stand how ridiculous I am.”

“Don’t say that, Father.”

At that moment, Namgung Do-Wei spoke firmly.

“Winning or losing a battle isn’t important. What is important is not winning the battle, but winning the war.”

“....”

“No matter what twists and turns you go through, if you can win in the end, it is never a defeat. If I were not of the Namgung family bloodline, I would have laughed at the attitude of Father, not the crisis of Namgung.”

“Do-Wei!”

Namgung Myung shouted in surprise, but Namgung Hwang laughed instead when he heard that.

“Right. Namgung Hwang of the Heavens is crying.”

Namgung Hwang, who had been laughing out loud, gritted his teeth and said.

“There will always be an opportunity. If we can endure this siege, the moment will surely come when we can throw them all into the cold riverbed.”

“That will be the case, Father.”

“Do-Wei.”

“Yes, Lord.”

Namgung Hwang gave a firm order.

“Encourage the men to not waver.”

“Don’t worry, Lord. Namgung’s Sword is by no means weak. If they had been so heartbroken, they wouldn’t have dared to use the name of Namgung.”

Namgung Hwang curled the corners of his lips and nodded.

His son had grown so much and was becoming his strength. Wasn’t every word from him even more like a Lord?

“Blue Sky Leader.”

“Yes, Lord.”

“Help the young lord.”

Namgung Myung nodded.

“That will definitely happen.”

Namgung Myung and Namgung Do-Wei bowed deeply and left. Namgung Hwang, who was left alone on the riverbank, quietly turned his head and looked at the ships surrounding the island.

‘Will we have to endure till Shaolin comes to help us?’

Namgung Hwang twisted his lips.

‘Myung is also good at lying.’

No, in fact, this could not be called a lie. That was just a true statement.

There was simply something he didn’t mention.

If Shaolin became aware of the situation and began to act, then the group of traitors would also mobilize.

What if the Hegemony Lord and the Ten Thousand People Clan reached the Yangtze River before Shaolin?

‘At that time, this will be my and the Namgung family’s grave.’

The Hegemony Lord would never miss such an opportunity.

“Haha.”

He laughed self-deprecatingly and glared at the Black Dragon Ship in the distance.

“Even if I die here, I will take your head with me, Black Dragon King!”

The force he emitted spread along the waves of the Yangtze River.

And the force was clearly conveyed to the Black Dragon King, who was observing the island from the river.

“Namgung Hwang.”

A smug smile appeared at the corner of the Black Dragon King’s mouth.

The force Namgung Hwang exuded now was indeed threatening, even more so than when he had allied with him three years ago.

But that was all. A beast is most ferocious when caught in a trap. The momentum only reflects Namgung Hwang’s current situation.

“Have you contacted the Alliance Lord?”

“Yes, Black Dragon King!”

“Nice.”

A bitter smile formed at the corner of the Black Dragon King’s mouth.

“Then where... shall we slowly cook the rat in the poison?”

An uncontrollable laugh escaped his lips.

‘I will teach you exactly what it means to have your blood run dry, Emperor’s Sword.’

Soon, Namgung’s blood will dye the river red.

“Mount Hua’s Sword Sainttt!”

As Hong Dae-Kwang burst through the door, Chung Myung’s eyes immediately sharpened.

“No, but does this man think this is some kind of beggar’s front yard?”

“This isn’t the time to be talking about that! Mount Hua’s Sword Saint!”

“What, again?”

“The Namgung family is—”

At the mention of the Namgung family, the eyes of those sitting around the White Blossom Hall narrowed simultaneously.

“The Namgung family is surrounded by pirates!”

“...”

When Chung Myung didn’t show any particular reaction, Hong Dae-Kwang raised his voice in frustration and moved closer, nearly nose-to-nose.

“The main force of the family is trapped on Plum Blossom Island and surrounded by pirate ships! If we don’t act now, they might all be wiped out. Are you even listening to—”

“Ah! You are making such disgusting noises; shut it!”

Chung Myung pushed Hong Dae-Kwang’s cheek away with his fingertips. If anyone else had rushed at him, he would have thrown a punch immediately, but since it was the beggar from the street, he seemed to want to minimize contact.

Mount Hua’s disciples nodded with pleased expressions.

“Chung Myung is polite. He usually uses his hands.”

“Yeah, I thought he would push him away with his feet.”

“He’s become much more well-behaved. If it were before, he would have kicked him with the sole of his shoe. I am happy with the change.”

“...”

Excuse me? Was this being polite?

What kind of darkness was Mount Hua experiencing? Hong Dae-Kwang wondered for a moment, but that wasn’t his main concern.

“W-what do we do?”

“What?”

“The Namgung family! The Namgung family!”

Chung Myung looked at Hong Dae-Kwang with a bewildered expression.

“What about the Namgung family?”

“Don’t we have to do something about it?”

“About the Namgung family?”

“Yes!”

“Why me?”

Hong Dae-Kwang’s eyes trembled.

“I told you they are all going to die. If this continues, aren’t we going to be wiped out?”

“Oh my. I see. So what should I do? Tsk tsk.”

Hong Dae-kwang’s mouth slowly opened.

“That...”

He tried to say something more, but before he could, Chung Myung snapped an annoyed look.

“But does this man think I am some kind of goblin? If there is a problem, you come running and whine at me!”

“...”

“If they thoughtlessly went in and got surrounded, they should have taken care of it themselves. They are not some three-year-old kids. They are the ones who cause the accidents, and I am the one who solves them?”

“Well, that is right, but even so...”

“That is why they were crawling in there in the first place. If that person is going to go insane, they have to go insane nicely. Where’s the place that they thought they were walking into so boldly? They say that when a croaker jumps in, a loach jumps in too. Do you think they can do it if I do it?”

He could understand why Namgung Hwang was mistaken.

Three years ago, in terms of strength alone, Mount Hua wasn’t even comparable to the Namgung family. However, since they saw that not even Mount Hua, but a part of Green Forest, occupied the island and simultaneously controlled the Southern lands and the pirates, they must have thought that it was possible.

‘Of course, it might look like that on the outside.’

However, Plum Blossom Island’s reality wasn’t like that. Plum Blossom Island was the result of a treaty that Jang Ilso and Chung Myung made out of mutual benefits.

It wasn’t a place where they could easily defend themselves and block the pirates’ attacks, but rather a place where they didn’t need to block the attacks because they didn’t attack.

The moment Mount Hua retreated and the treaty vanished, everything changed. Plum Blossom Island was now nothing more than a dangerous place in the middle of pirate territory with no escape route. If they could occupy it, they would be guaranteed huge profits, but it meant that it was impossible to occupy in the first place.

Let’s say that the Four Precepts Alliance and the Nine Great Sects fought over the island.

Then which side would have the advantage?

It was a land that the Four Precept Alliance, who controlled the river, had no choice but to take.

That was why Chung Myung and Im So-Byeong withdrew before the war broke out.

But they entered that dangerous land without thinking...

If they were weak, they would get hit; if they didn’t know, they would get hit; if they acted ignorant, they would get hit... ah, maybe not the last one.

In any case, wasn’t it the truth of the martial arts world that if they were ignorant, they would get hit?

“But back in the old days, the Namgung family had quite a few ideas.”

Usually, it was the Peng family who caused trouble, and the Namgung family was the one who cleaned it up. But what happened on land in the past hundred years that made the Namgung family look like this...

“Wait.”

“Uh?”

“I am asking just in case, but there is that man, Namgung Hwang.”

“Uh? Namgung family lord? Why Namgung family lord?”

“Who is that man’s mother?”

At that moment, Hong Dae-Kwang’s eyes grew so wide that they couldn’t get any bigger.

“M-Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon. O-of course, I know you aren’t so rough, but this is a bit too much....”

“Ugh, shit!”

Chung Myung kicked Hong Dae-Kwang out of the blue.

“This man is suddenly making people weird! No matter what, do I look like someone who would do that?”

The disciples of Mount Hua who heard that all nodded, satisfied.

“That is possible.”

“And more, too.”

“He can do it as easily as breathing! This much is nothing.”

But these people?

“That isn’t it. Where is his maternal lineage? Family! Her family!”

“Ah, family?”

Hong Dae-Kwang sighed in relief and searched his mind.

“Let’s see... From what I know, the mother of the current Namgung family lord is Peng...”

“Ah, right?”

Right. That had to be it.

Where would that blood go?

Only then did Chung Myung speak with a refreshed expression, as if his unsolved question had been resolved.

“Somehow, I wondered how such a man came from the Namgung family.”

It could happen in a normal sect. A sect was a place where many people gathered with a single virtue.

However, a family was connected by blood. If people with similar temperaments received similar education, they would eventually become similar, right?

Of course, even considering that Namgung Hwang had a mixed temperament from the Peng family, it was a bit too much...

“Chung Myung.”

“Uh?”

At that moment, Baek Cheon, one of the Five Swords, approached Chung Myung and asked.

“What do you think will happen? Do you think the Namgung family will be able to escape from there?”

“It will be difficult.”

“...Then what happens now?”

“Hmm. That is...”

Chung Myung rubbed his chin.

“It went as expected, but it is also a little different.”

It was obvious that the Namgung family would suffer a complete defeat. The warriors of this era had never experienced such a large-scale war as this. As a result, they did not understand how important terrain was.

Of course, they must have learned military strategy. However, they must have thought that they could overcome it with their own force.

“I predicted that the Namgung family would advance to Plum Blossom Island... the problem is the Black Dragon King.”

“Black Dragon King?”

“He looks like he will rush into Plum Blossom Island and say, ‘you die, or I die’... he is surprisingly experienced. Like an old raccoon.”

“You shouldn’t judge a person by their appearance, but...”

Baek Cheon also agreed with that. The Black Dragon King they saw in the Yangtze River three years ago looked like the embodiment of hegemony. The pirates led by him and the Namgung family led by Namgung Hwang clashed in battle, but who would have imagined that it would turn out as such?“

“If that Black Dragon King is as violent as we think, and we invade the island to annihilate them while they are surrounded, then the Namgung family will have a chance.”

Baek Cheon’s face hardened as he heard those words. Whenever Chung Myung said something like that, he always had a different situation in mind.

“If.”

Baek Cheon said in a darkened voice.

“What if the Black Dragon King and the pirates didn’t plan on invading the island? Would it become a fight over which side’s rescue would arrive first?”

“If one thinks about it logically, that is what would happen. But... it won’t happen as such.”

“Uh? Why?”

“The Black Dragon King must know that fact.”

“What is that...?”

Baek Cheon tilted his head for a moment, then flinched as if something occurred to him.

“That... right. True.”

Jo Gul, who didn’t understand the talks between the two, looked at them with puzzling eyes and asked.

“Sasuk. What are you talking about? Please explain it so I can understand.”

“...The Black Dragon King, who is surrounding the Namgung family, will also know that it is disadvantageous for him for the rescue team to arrive.”

“Right. That man is no moron either.”

“But think about it. If the Black Dragon King thinks that the rescue team from the Nine Great Sects will arrive first, would he just let the poisoned rat out?”

“...Of course not.”

Baek Cheon slightly bit his lip.

“At the very least, he will try to reduce the Namgung family’s power as much as possible before the rescue team arrives. That means they won’t just waste time facing each other.”

Chung Myung nodded.

“And the position of the Black Dragon King is different. If the Four Sects Alliance arrives first, it means the destruction of the Namgung family. Still, if the Nine Great Sect arrives first, it’s just a bit more troublesome for the Pirates.”

“Because it’s on the river?”

“Yes.”

Chung Myung’s lips curled up.

In the past, when they had these kinds of talks, Chung Myung’s mouth would normally be busy explaining things. But now, Baek Cheon understands the situation first and then answers without questioning thoughtlessly.

“Well, all the Namgung family can do is hold on. That’s all they can do right now.”

“Can they hold on, though?”

“Namgung Hwang is insane, but he is no idiot. He should be able to endure it. The problem is...“

Chung Myung’s face twisted slightly.

He paused for a moment as if choosing his words, then slowly said,

“Do you know when a person falls?”

“...”

It was such a sudden remark, but Baek Cheon couldn’t bring himself to speak. He could feel an inexplicable heaviness in the voice.

“The moment a person falls isn’t when things get hard. You can endure hardships, you know. When there is hope that you can survive, you hold on.”

“...”

“A person falls when the hope is cut off.”

Chung Myung sneered.

“I wonder about it too. I wonder if that man who calculates war is willing to risk his life to save the Namgung family.”

He didn’t mention the name, but everyone there knew who Chung Myung was referring to. No, they couldn’t help but know.

“I-it cannot be...”

“It’s just a thought, a thought, you know. Nothing has happened yet.”

Chung Myung waved his hand as if to tell him not to overthink. But unlike the calm and light waving, his eyes were dark and sunken.

“But if...”

Chung Myung paused and continued,

“If, in a thousand outcomes, what I think does happen...”

The atmosphere turned heavy.

“The Namgung family will live to see hell unfold.”

There was silence for a moment. No one could even speak at that.

“Well, we can just stay shut and have rice cakes.”

“Chung Myung, shouldn’t we do something?”

“Uh?”

“If the Namgung family is in danger....”

“Sasuk.”

Chung Myung cut off Baek Cheon’s words, crushing any hope.

“The moment you step into battle, you need to take responsibility for all your decisions.”

“....”

“People who think it’s natural for them to step forward and help will also think that someone else will step forward and help when they are in danger. That complacency will end up costing their lives.”

Baek Cheon shut his mouth.

“That is a battlefield, that is a war. Don’t forget it. If you don’t want to see, the sahyung-sajaes die because of that complacent judgment.”

“... I will keep it in mind.”

Chung Myung nodded and shrugged.

“Everyone watch carefully. It might happen to us someday.”

Everyone gulped, feeling a burning sensation in their throats.

“Abbot.”

The abbot didn’t respond to the call. There were times when he always wore a gentle smile, but now he remained silent, his face cold and hard, as if he had forgotten how to smile.

It couldn’t be helped.

He had left with grand boasts, but he returned without any success from Mount Hua, an extremely humiliating experience for him.

He couldn’t even bear to look at the faces of those who had been waiting for his return to Shaolin.

“Namgung is surrounded.”

The news he received in the midst of this rendered the abbot silent.

No, perhaps it would be more accurate to say he was shocked. It was as if his mind had gone blank for a moment.

Of all possible scenarios, the worst outcome was thrust upon him.

“Tsk.”

The Gentle Crow Beggar, who was watching the abbot, clicked his tongue.

It was a habitual act, not malicious, but that short clicking sound twisted the abbot’s insides.

“It would have been better not to waste time and go straight to Yangtze.”

“...”

It was unclear whether those words were a subtle sarcastic remark towards the abbot. But did it matter to the abbot? At this moment, he was more ashamed and afraid of being criticized for his mistakes than anything else in the world.

“... Amitabha.”

After a long, long silence, the words he mumbled amounted to nothing more than a short prayer.

Peng Yeop, who had been waiting silently for a response, finally spoke with a slightly annoyed tone.

“This isn’t the time for this. If the retreat is cut off, all they can do is hold out, and if the Four Precepts Alliance arrives first, the name of Namgung might vanish from the murim world.”

“Hmm.”

Someone’s heavy, hushed voice echoed in agreement.

“We must go to the Yangtze River immediately.”

Even Jong Ri-Hyung, who often opposed Peng Yeop, didn’t refute him this time. This was proof of how urgent and frightening the situation was.

“Abbot.”

The abbot, who had been meditating with his eyes closed, opened them at Peng Yeop’s prompting.

“... Let us do so.”

A bright blue light emanated from the abbot’s eyes.

“Now that the situation has escalated to this point, there can be no more hesitation. I will lead all the monks of Shaolin to the Yangtze River. So everyone here, please mobilize the power of each sect.”

Jong Ri-Hyung asked in shock.

“A-Abbot. Are you planning on going to war?”

“... If it is necessary, there is no reason to avoid it, right?”

“But...”

It was clear what Jong Ri-Hyung wanted to say.

Going to war with the Four Precepts Alliance would be nearly suicidal. Even if they were fortunate enough to win, the benefits would be minimal and would primarily favor other sects.

However, despite understanding these concerns, the abbot seemed resolute this time, with no intention of backing down.

“I will send letters to each sect again and ask for support.”

“... Will those who have backed out once change their minds now?”

“Since the situation has changed, they will have to make a choice too.”

“....”

Jong Ri-Hyung fell silent.

He sensed that the abbot’s tone was different from before. It wasn’t particularly coercive, but it felt strangely uncomfortable to engage with him now.

“... If even the Tang Family steps up, it would be a bit easier.”

Jong Ri-Hyung’s grumble made the abbot twitch.

“What difference would it make if we sought help from those who aren’t around?”

“That is true, but...”

“You all should know by now.”

The abbot rebuked him with cold eyes.

“The reason I have endured all this time was to resolve things in the natural order, and it wasn’t because Shaolin lacked any strength. But when things go this badly, sometimes you have to force things to work out.”

It might not be wrong.

But given the situation, it was also true that the words meant nothing. If only he had made the decision a little earlier, things wouldn’t have been as they were.

The abbot spoke in a sharp voice.

“We will head to the Yangtze River without delay. Speed is the most important thing now.”

He had no choice. All of this was the result of the Namgung family’s arbitrary decision. Still, if Shaolin truly claimed to be the leader of the murim, they had to accept that everything was Shaolin’s responsibility.

If Namgung were to be isolated and defeated at the Yangtze River, public sentiment towards the Nine Great Sects would truly fall into the abyss. At least Namgung would have fought the Evil Sects, but the Nine Great Sects would end up as those ignoring them.

“Please hurry. We must arrive before the Hegemony Lord reaches the river!”

“Is it possible?”

When Jong Ri-Hyung asked worriedly, the abbot nodded.

“It is completely possible. Isn’t it much closer here than in Guangxi, where the Ten Thousand People are? Even if the Hegemony Lord immediately moves after hearing the news, they won’t be able to arrive before us.”

Jong Ri-Hyung nodded as if he had a point.

“If you think about it the other way, it’s like the Namgung family holding the pirates. It is hard to wipe out the pirates scattered over the Yangtze River. However, if they are all gathered in one place, it is a great chance. If the Namgung family and both sides can attack the pirates at this chance, the victory would come in an instant.”

The abbot shouted firmly in a majestic voice.

“So let us move right away.”

Peng Yeop responded immediately.

“Yes.”

Jong Ri-Hyung still looked slightly unsure, but since he couldn’t back out in this situation, he ended up agreeing. However, he didn’t forget to make one request.

“Abbot, I will follow what you say, but you must also consider the possibility of the situation not being favorable. I believe that you will definitely draw out support from the other sects.”

At those words, the abbot turned his head and looked at Jong Ri-Hyung.

“...”

A moment of silence followed. Soon, an emotionless voice pierced his ears.

“I will.”

The answer was clearly a positive one, but Jong Ri-Hyung had a hard time shaking off the feeling that the response from a moment ago wasn’t a good one.

But how could he confirm it? In the end, he had no choice but to answer with an anxious heart.

“... I will join the group.”

The abbot’s gaze turned to Gentle Crow Beggar, and he nodded.

“You can take the answer as such. There is no time, so please return to the sect right away, finish the preparations, and then gather at the entrance of the city.”

“Yes, Abbot.”

“We don’t have time! Hurry!”

“Yes!”

“Then.”

Those who were sitting suddenly got up and hurriedly walked out. Finally, Gentle Crow Beggar, who lifted himself off the ground, took a reluctant step and turned his head.

He saw the abbot sitting in place with his eyes closed.

‘When it was time to make a decision, he hesitated endlessly, but when it was time to be careful, he decided on an answer without thought and moved ahead.’

He didn’t evaluate the abbot as someone bad. As the leader of Shaolin and a great abbot in the murim world, he had fulfilled his role well so far. Indeed, he had successfully mediated conflicts between the sects with his gentle personality.

But.

‘They say a good king in good times can turn to a tyrant in troubled times.’

As the world began to change rapidly, it felt like the strengths of the abbot were not being utilized at all.

He wanted to stop the abbot.

But there was just one reason why he couldn’t oppose the decision.

‘This has to be hell.’

The tragedy of the Namgung family could be surmised.

‘Please hold on, Namgung family lord.’

The Gentle Crow beggar, known for his filial nature, ran towards the Beggars Union main hall like the wind.

Namgung Myung looked down at the person who had fallen in front of him. His face gradually twisted into an expression of rage.

The person who had died with his eyes wide open was someone he knew very well.

All the members of the Namgung family were like kin to him. But now, the first victim among those he considered family had appeared.

“This is...!”

He gritted his teeth in anger.

It felt as if the poisoned blade stuck in the side of the corpse was stabbing his eyes.

Nine times. That was the number of times they had been attacked in the past three days.

The culprits?

None.

No one got hurt, no one died. The attackers emerged from the river, attempting to assault those guarding the area, only to end up dying themselves.

It was so absurd it bordered on trivial. It was a result that confirmed Namgung Hwang’s assertion that there was no reason to get caught in the water where your feet touched the ground.

But this was the only outcome. If everything had gone so smoothly, Namgung Myung wouldn’t have been so angry now.

When they repelled the first attack, everyone’s courage soared, and their morale rose endlessly. After stopping the second attack, there was a suggestion that instead of this, they should try to approach the pirate’s ship and attack it.

And when they finally repelled the third attack, Namgung Myung realized.

During the two days of attacks, not a single person had slept properly.

Yet the attacks continued.

Sometimes they took advantage of the darkness of night, and sometimes they came in broad daylight.

And that wasn’t all. When the morning dew settled when the deep sunset dyed the river....

The attacks continued regardless of the time.

The Namgung Family’s swordsmen bravely repelled their attacks one after another. Still, the attack that had just occurred had finally resulted in the first casualty.

“... Empty one of the warehouses and move the body.”

“Leader. First, let us bury....”

Namgung Myung’s sharp gaze suddenly turned.

“Will his soul rest in the land of pirates?”

“... I am sorry.”

Namgung Myung gritted his teeth and said.

“If we endure for a few days, we will be able to escape the island. Then we can collect the bodies and leave. So do as I say.”

“Yes.”

He pressed his eyes hard as he watched the comrades move the bodies.

‘Damn it.’

They were not strong opponents. No, they were nothing more than trash.

And yet, there were casualties.

Because they were pushed to the limit by continuous attacks, they ended up making mistakes that they would never have made under normal circumstances.

This island was large. It was large enough for hundreds of people to live there at the same time.

But at the same time, this island was narrow. If the pirates set foot on it, they could run all the way to the other side of the island in no time.

In other words, they were currently trapped in a pavilion with a collapsed wall, guarding against invasion by enemies.

‘No, that situation would be a hundred times better.’

In that case, at least they would be able to know where the enemy was.

But they had no idea where pirates would attack from. Maybe the pirates were waiting for a chance even in the waters that Namgung Myung was looking at.

So, no one could rest easily. They didn’t know when the pirates would attack and when their defenses would be breached, and they would storm into the island.

Even though they knew they needed to rest, the tension that had reached its limit didn’t allow them even a moment of relaxation.

And then...

Kwaaang!

“Damn it!”

“Why are you shooting useless things like that, those bastards!”

The pirate ships surrounding the island occasionally approached stealthily and fired their arrows. Of course, the trained Namgung family swordsmen would not be hit by projectiles flying from a distance.

But this was an island where there was no place to hide.

If they were careful, they could avoid it, but if not, it was no wonder they would die. In such a situation, who could even relax?
