Why did he waste time when it was going to end up like this? He came out and talked like it was a great plan, but didn’t he end up losing men in the process?

The Black Dragon King glared at the Yangtze River with gleaming eyes.

He saw a small ship slowly moving through the waves. It looked so slow, as if it had nothing to do with the brutal massacre happening right before its eyes.

“... damn it!”

What made him angrier was not Jang Ilso, who had twisted things for him. It was he who dared not ask about the damage to the pirates, even though Jang Ilso had brought so much mess his way.

Clench.

Clearly, their relationship had not been like this when he arrived at the Yangtze River. Where on earth did things go so wrong?

The Black Dragon King’s eyes were filled with ferocious anger.

The surging anger had to be released somehow.

“Tear them all apart and kill them! All of them! Don’t kill them easily! Let them die feeling the bitter price for daring to fight against the pirates!”

His anger poured out wildly towards the poor Namgung family.

Crack!

The sharply sharpened harpoon, which had qi around it, pierced a body.

“Kuak...”

A lowly pleasure filled the pirate’s eyes as he saw the victim’s bloodied clothes turning even more bloody.

The pirate twisted the harpoon he had thrust in. No matter how great a man is, the moment the harpoon goes into the stomach and rips out the intestines, they will squirm in pain and collapse.

But.

“Uh...”

The swordsman of the Namgung family, who had been pierced by the harpoon, suddenly smiled. The moment those blood-soaked teeth were revealed, the iron sword in his hand cut through the air like a ray of light.

Paaat!

Slash!

The sound of the sword cutting through the bone resonated dully, and the head of the pirate who had been cut off soared into the air.

The swordsman, who had been staggering ahead as if he would fall, supported his body with the bloody sword placed on the ground and lifted his head.

“Cough!”

The one who had stabbed him with the harpoon was now a headless corpse, but the harpoon was still embedded in his stomach. The swordsman swung his sword without hesitation and cut off the shaft of the harpoon.

Kakang!

Cutting the shaft short was a double-edged sword.

It was better for moving his body right now, but the pointed edge in his body would keep digging itself deeper into his flesh with each movement.

But what did it matter?

Since death was all that awaited him, what difference did it make if there were a few harpoons stuck in his corpse?

Two harpoons were already stuck in his stomach. All of his left hand’s fingers were cut off, and he no longer felt any pain from the deep cut on his shoulder. The deepest wound was on his thigh, with blood flowing down like a stream from a severed blood vessel.

But that didn’t matter either.

‘This isn’t enough.’

His eyes were filled with venom.

‘The lord died in a much more miserable way!’

He wouldn’t fall until he had at least 10 harpoons stuck in his body. If his life held nothing but death, he must endure one more attack from the person next to him or behind him.

Swish!

Through his blurred vision, he caught a glimpse of harpoons flying towards him. Not one, but two... no, five?

It would be useless even if he knew. No matter how many harpoons there were, it was impossible to block them anyway.

This Namgung swordsman, whose name was Namgung Pyo, simply stepped on the ground and swung his sword. It was an attack that ignored defense. No matter how many harpoons pierced his body, he was determined to drag the pirates who were piercing him down to hell.

Crack! Crack! Slash!

The sound of the harpoons piercing his body and the sound of the sword cutting through flesh rang out simultaneously.

“...”

Namgung Pyo’s body slowly collapsed as he felt cold metal digging into his throat.

The world darkened, and death approached.

For a moment, he thought it was fortunate that his neck was pierced because he could no longer scream out.

Thud.

The feeling of the ground touching his body was dull and faint. He stretched out as he felt the world gradually being covered in darkness.

It was comfortable.

It was so comfortable that he just wanted to rest like that, just as such...

But at that moment, Namgung Pyo opened his eyes wide. He grabbed the sword he had half let go of. The pirates, who had already thought him dead, trampled and advanced.

Namgung Pyo swung his sword while lying face down, revealing his blood-soaked teeth.

“ACKKKKKK!”

“This bastard!”

The unexpected attack from the ground made the pirates fall, clutching their legs. Namgung Pyo snickered at the sight. No, he tried to laugh.

Crack! Crack!

But he couldn’t. He had turned into a hedgehog after being pierced by dozens of harpoons, so there was no room for him to laugh.

‘Young lord... please continue...’

Thud.

Another life seeped into the river.

“EUKKKKK!”

The Namgung family’s swordsmen were like ghosts.

War wasn’t a state that could be changed by will alone. What advanced the war was not will, but effort.

But now, the Namgung family swordsmen were shaking up the common sense of the murim.

Even with a harpoon lodged in their bodies or a spear in their heads, they swung their swords. Even when pierced, they didn’t back down.

The only thing that came out of their mouths was a vicious roar.

“DIE! Damn Evil Sect!”

“I will show you how the Namgung swordsmen die!”

The pirates prided themselves on being steadfast. They had clearly cornered the Namgung family like frightened dogs in the last battle.

But at this moment, they were overwhelmed by Namgung’s swordsmen, who were so few in number.

Could there be another scene that fits the saying “Rushing in Without Regard For Death”?

Some swung their swords while holding their stomach contents with one hand, those who held broken swords with their bare hands and swung them madly, and those who cut their opponents’ ankles even after falling.

It was clear that the word ‘hell’ was prepared for such a moment.

And in the middle of this hell, Namgung Myung was swinging his sword like a madman.

Crack.

A harpoon stuck in his shoulder with fierce momentum. But Namgung Myung didn’t blink and cut the heart of the enemy standing in front of him with his sword.

“COME!”

Contrary to his spirited shout, his heart was filled with sorrow.

He didn’t care about giving up his life. He was already supposed to die. When Namgung Hwang gave up his life in the river, he was the one who should have opened the way and died first.

The only reason he survived this miserable life was because of Namgung Hwang’s orders.

But...

“Cough!”

Every time the suppressed groans pierced his ears, it felt like his heart was being torn out each time. The groans of those biting down on their tongues and dying to not show weakness to the pirates were more painful than screams and more sorrowful than crying.

“EUKKKK!”

A wicked scream burst from Namgung Myung’s mouth. His sword, now deviating from the Namgung family sword technique, began to carve out new paths.

One day, a warrior would die by holding a sword. Was there anyone here who didn’t have that resolve?

But...

‘It is sad.’

Before he knew it, tears were welling up in Namgung Myung’s eyes.

In this lonely place where no one had reached out to them, those who were dying without even screaming met such sad and sorrowful ends that, despite their efforts to hold back, tears fell.

Would the world understand such sadness?

Would the world remember their deaths?

“Kuaak...”

A sound, part groan, part cry, escaped from Namgung Myung’s mouth.

“Kuak.. euk...”

A groan escaped from the mouth of a Namgung swordsman with a large sword stuck in his chest. Another, whose arm was pierced and could no longer hold a sword, discarded the weapon he had wielded his entire life and lunged to grab the pirate in front of him, clinging on.

To save even one person behind him, even for just one moment.

Namgung Myung laughed like a madman.

“Euh...”

Overwhelming.

They were already surrounded on all sides. No matter how fiercely they swung their swords, the number of pirates did not diminish. More attackers rushed in fiercely than they could kill.

No matter how much they cursed and braced themselves, they couldn’t do this alone.

‘Are you watching this?’

Namgung Myung turned his head blankly towards the riverside. Towards Shaolin and the Nine Great Sects, who must be watching their end from afar.

“Still... are you still going to watch? You bastards!”

Crack! Crack!

As his sword swung in a fit of rage, slicing through a pirate’s body, several harpoons flew toward him.

“Leaderrr!”

The harpoons flew towards his head at incredible speed.

“Protect him!”

“Protect the leader!”

The Blue Sky Swordsmen rushed in, using their bodies to protect Namgung Myung. They swung their swords desperately to deflect the harpoons, extending their arms to take the wounds meant for Namgung Myung.

Namgung Myung screamed.

“What are you doing! Morons!”

They shouldn’t die like that. They shouldn’t die so fleetingly. They shouldn’t die so miserably in a place like this.

Someone would become the hero of Namgung, and someone else would become the Greatest Swordsman in the world. At the very least, they could become someone’s father and someone’s best friend.

Then why did they have to die so vainly?

“Eu-ukkk...”

He tried to rush ahead, cursing, but his leg, pierced by the harpoon, wouldn’t budge. Having poured all his qi into the injured people for the past few days, he was the most exhausted person there.

He collapsed on the spot, and his eyes could see it: the sight of dozens of harpoons piercing the backs of those blocking the path to him.

“Uh...”

The blood splashed as the ones pierced by harpoons got pushed back.

The moment the hot blood poured down on his face, he thought he was going to get burned from it. But maybe, it was really hot...

“Euk...”

Namgung Myung’s head, which had been laughing, sobbing in despair, and then tearing at his own hair, suddenly slumped forward like a criminal ready to be beheaded.

He tried to be dignified. He wanted to stay as such until the very end.

But... it was now an impossible thing for Namgung Myung.

‘Lord. I am...’

No more tears flowed down his face.

‘... couldn’t protect the Namgung family.’

People were all the same.

When there was something that could not be achieved by one’s own strength, something so desperately desired, one simply wished for it.

And so, he closed his eyes, bowed his head, and held the hands instead of the sword, and just desperately...

‘Please, someone...’

A deep sob escaped Namgung Myung’s mouth.

“Some... Someone please help...”

In a voice that cracked like metal scraping.

“Please....”

He cried out more desperately than any other scream.

However, his feelings could be conveyed to the pirates. A pirate approached Namgung Myung, who had collapsed on the spot, begging for help, laughing.

“Fucking moron.”

Clank!

The harpoon in the hand moved.

“Help? The name of Namgung must be wailing. Since Shaolin has abandoned you, do you think there will be anyone there who will help you?”

He must have had some sort of position among the pirates, so when he started to walk, the other pirates followed.

“No matter how much you speak of justice and harmony, this is your true nature.”

The moment he heard those words, Namgung Myung’s shoulders trembled.

“Do not worry. Everyone here will die, so your journey to the afterlife will not be so lonely.”

With a deep mockery of their situation, the pirate pulled the harpoon in hand. As if he wanted to pierce the head of Namgung Myung in a single blow.

“Just die!”

It was the moment when the harpoon, which had been pulled back tight, shot towards Namgung Myung’s head.

Paaaat!

A tremendous cracking sound that seemed to burst the eardrums erupted. The one who had thrown the harpoon turned his gaze to the size in shock.

‘What was that?’

What he saw was a giant disc.

No!

It was flying towards him, spinning fiercely like a disc...

‘Sword?’

Kwaaang!

His thoughts stopped there.

He clearly saw a sword stuck in the sandbank of Plum Blossom Island, creating a huge hole.

“...”

He was about to contort his face at the absurd sight. A single line appeared from the tip of his head to his groin.

Everyone stared blankly at the sight.

“Ugh...”

Sensing the change in the body, the pirate touched his face with trembling hands.

‘Blood?’

He felt his palms wet with red blood. The pirate’s eyes trembled at it. The blood soaking his hands increased in volume. And soon blood began to flow down his chin.

“No... no way...”

Shock and disbelief.

But that was all.

The two wide-open eyes began to look up and down, and soon the body of the pirate split open in two and collapsed to the ground.

Thud.

In an instant, a cold silence took over Plum Blossom Island.

The air, which had been so heated with madness, suddenly cooled down, and it was a shocking sight. Those who had been blankly watching turned their gazes toward one place as if they all knew.

Towards the place where the sword had cut through the wide river and saved Namgung Myung.

And there he stood.

A man on the riverbank, where the blue waves of the Yangtze River surged like waves.

An unbearable groan escaped Namgung Myung’s mouth.

“Ahh... ah...”

How could he not know?

That black robe.

The red pattern on the left chest, like a single stream of blood flowing from the heart.

Even from a distance, with his blurred vision, the figure was strangely clear.

“Mount Hua....”

The name finally escaped Namgung Myung’s mouth.

“Mount Hua’s Sword Saint....”

Chung Myung glared at the ships floating on the Yangtze River and Plum Blossom Island with dark eyes.

Everyone remained silent in that situation.

A man watching from a large ship, a little far from Plum Blossom Island, curled his bloody red lips like a waning moon.

“As expected...”

Jang Ilso smiled happily.

“I told you not to disappoint me.”

If someone were to describe the scene here, what would they say?

No matter how skilled they were at speaking, they would struggle to find the words.

Was it because the situation was difficult to convey?

No. It was because no one would believe it unless they saw it with their own eyes.

Consider this.

The faces here were incredibly illustrious. Each person was capable of shaking the murim world with just their presence. Nearby was Shaolin, known as the best sect in the world, and far away was the ruler of the Southern Lands, leader of the Four Precepts Alliance, and the Ten Thousand People Clan.

And Kongtong and the Yangtze River pirates, who guarded them, were also renowned worldwide.

How could one describe the sight of these great figures, who have come to rule the world with their fame alone, falling silent at the appearance of one young swordsman?

Mount Hua’s Sword Saint. That name was certainly significant. The title he earned against none other than Jang Ilso and the Four Precepts Alliance held a unique quality, incomparable to other titles.

But that was all.

No matter how great the name of Mount Hua’s Sword Saint was, it was absolutely impossible to silence all those gathered here. No, it was impossible not just for Mount Hua’s Sword Saint, but for anyone else in the world.

That was what they came to understand.

The fact that a person’s presence was not determined by their reputation.

His dark, clear eyes slowly scanned the Yangtze River. Following those eyes, everyone present on the Yangtze River held their breath.

However, even in silence, they didn’t know why they were so captivated by his every move.

Chung Myung’s slowly moving eyes soon fixed on one place.

The Plum Blossom Island.

Right where Namgung’s survivors were fighting fiercely.

“Ah...”

Namgung Myung’s body trembled.

There he was.

He was just one man, but he came running for them.

Nothing might change. This war could not be undone with the power of one person.

And even if he wasn’t alone, it would be the same. Now, even if someone came, they would not be able to cross the Yangtze River and save themselves.

So, it would be too much to hope for Mount Hua’s Sword Saint to save them.

But.

‘... this much is enough.’

Everyone in the world had abandoned them. They had turned their backs on them.

Perhaps what truly drove them into despair was not their situation, but the horrible loneliness of not having anyone to reach out to.

But at least, there was one person. Someone who ran over to save them. As if to prove that the path they had walked on wasn’t completely wrong.

That alone was enough for Namgung Myung.

It was at the very moment when Namgung Myung was looking at Chung Myung with reddened eyes.

“Damned fools.”

The Black Dragon King, who had climbed onto the bow of the Black Dragon Ship, spoke coldly in a heavy voice. The bandits who heard that voice trembled like puppies that had just met a tiger.

The Black Dragon King, who had glared at the bandits with boiling eyes, turned his head and looked directly at Chung Myung.

“Little bastard.”

And he gritted his teeth.

For a moment, that little swordsman’s presence surpassed everyone around. Right there in the Yangtze River. That was an unbearable humiliation for the Black Dragon King, the leader of the 18 Yangtze Riverways.

If he hadn’t been humiliated by Jang Ilso earlier, he would have done something to overcome this, but having been oppressed by Jang Ilso once, he simply couldn’t bear this situation.

“What are you going to do?”

His voice, full of anger, exploded.

“The only thing you can do is vent your anger there. No matter what you do, nothing will change.”

It was a statement intended to suppress the opponent. But at the same time, it was a clear and cold fact being stated.

The situation was close to being done.

“Or are you planning to swim across the Yangtze River to this island? The path is already blocked, you moron!”

However, even after hearing those words, Chung Myung’s expression didn’t change at all. He simply stared at the Black Dragon King with a cold gaze.

That indifferent reaction twisted the Black Dragon King’s insides. Just as he was about to shout, Chung Myung’s mouth, which had been closed the whole time, slowly opened.

“I don’t know if you can understand it with that stupid head of yours....”

“... What?”

“Let me correct you on three things you said.”

Three things?

“First. Who is here alone?”

Step. Step.

At that moment, someone slowly walked up from under the mound on the riverbank where Chung Myung was standing.

A man with an impressive-looking black robe and a plum blossom pattern embroidered on the chest, with a white ribbon tied around his head.

Mount Hua’s Righteous Sword, Baek Cheon.

He walked slowly with cold, sunken eyes and stood next to Chung Myung.

His cold eyes, like ice, seemed to freeze all the water in the Yangtze River.

It wasn’t just Baek Cheon. The Plum Blossom Ice Sword, Yu Yiseol, approached Chung Myung and stood next to him.

Step. Step. Step.

Following her, they began to arrive one by one. Beyond one, ten, then a hundred swordsmen appeared with determined steps. Everyone’s eyes held the coldness of the world.

“...”

The sound of someone gulping felt like thunder to them.

Mount Hua.

Now, that name couldn’t be explained as a simple sect in Shaanxi. Mount Hua had finally arrived at the very Yangtze River.

“Young... Young lord...”

And then, Namgung Myung saw.

A man visible in the Mount Hua sect who was lined up with fierce momentum. The white robe dyed crimson with dried blood, but how could he not recognize that man?

Namgung Do-Wei.

He was standing there as if he were part of Mount Hua.

His clothes and complexion clearly showed how much he had suffered, but his sharp gaze was in no way inferior to that of Mount Hua.

The hundred or so swordsmen didn’t utter a single word, but glared at the pirates of Yangtze with cold eyes.

It was a truly terrifying sight. The intimidation that had never been seen in the past Mount Hua sect was now weighing down on the Yangtze River.

Everyone there saw Mount Hua during the past Yangtze River incident. That was why they knew.

The reason that the Mount Hua sect was feared was not because they were strong. It was because they were a sect that never backed down. It was that much of a burden to deal with a sect that would never back down, no matter the consequences.

Mount Hua was not the only one who had reached that place.

“It seems that it isn’t completely too late.”

The group of people in green robes revealed themselves.

If you don’t recognize them after seeing their distinctive wide-sleeved green robes, you don’t deserve to enjoy the world of murim.

“S-Sichuan Tang family!”

Tang Gunak, who appeared to be leading the Tang family, surveyed the Yangtze River with a stern face, as if he would remember those who died here.

The Namgung family’s swordsmen looked at Mount Hua and the Sichuan Tang family, and Namgung Do-Wei standing next to them, with trembling eyes.

The Namgung family’s young lord was there. Even though he was horribly injured, he had brought Mount Hua and the Tang family to save them. He looked like he would collapse and die at any moment.

Just that fact alone gave them a new fighting spirit. Everyone clenched their teeth. The young lord had suffered such great injuries and still refused to give up their life, so how could they easily throw their lives away and give up?

“And... second.”

Chung Myung glared at the Black Dragon King. At that moment, the body of the Black Dragon King trembled.

‘What, those eyes...’

This was the first time he had properly faced Chung Myung. In the previous war, he had only briefly watched him fight Jang Ilso. At that time, he had secretly ridiculed Jang Ilso for not being able to deal with even one young brat right away.

But now, the moment he looked into Chung Myung’s eyes, he realized it too.

That guy was not an ordinary person.

There was no way someone with eyes like that would be ordinary.

“Kid?”

A low laugh escaped Chung Myung’s mouth. It was a twisted laugh that seemed to openly mock or self-mock.

“A real kid does not know what war is and speaks just nonsense.”

Chung Myung stretched out his hand to the side. Then, the disciples of Mount Hua handed him a spare sword. Chung Myung, who received the sword and wore it on the waist sheath, twisted his lips and said.

“I will tell you, kid.”

Chung Myung’s mouth turned to a sly smile, revealing his pure white teeth.

“What a war really is.”

At that moment, goosebumps ran down the Black Dragon King’s back.

‘What?’

Why did those words, which could be considered a normal bluff, feel so eerie?

‘Damn it!’

The Black Dragon King bit his lips. It was a brief moment, but the fact that he was momentarily overwhelmed by that force tore his pride apart.

“Finally, the third one.”

Chung Myung laughed like the devil.

“You don’t find a path, you fucking moron. You make one!”

That was the moment.

The warriors of the Sichuan Tang family pulled their hands from under their sleeves. The Black Dragon King, who saw what they were holding, glared at them.

‘Wood blocks?’

The Tang family warriors rushed ahead, holding wooden blocks too big to hide in their sleeves.

And!

Paat!

As if they were assassin weapons, the wooden blocks were released from the hand and flew through the sky over the Yangtze River.

Thud! Thud! Thud! Thud!

At that moment, everyone on the Yangtze River witnessed the spectacle. The wooden blocks that the Tang family warriors had thrown onto the river’s surface settled without sinking, floating to provide a makeshift foothold.

Hundreds of these wooden blocks lined up, forming a single path that cut through the wide expanse of the Yangtze River.

This was the only path leading to their destination, Plum Blossom Island!

“Sasuk! Sago!”

Clang!

Baek Cheon and Yu Yiseol drew their swords.

“Break through the pirates!”

“Okay!”

“Yes!”

“Namgung Do-Wei!”

“Yes!”

“Clear the way! You will carve a path to Namgung with your own hands!”

Namgung Do-Wei responded with a face full of determination.

“Yes!”

There was no need for further explanation. They would understand what to do without additional instructions.

“Namgung Family!”

Chung Myung shouted until his throat was raw.

The Namgung family’s swordsmen gritted their teeth and looked at him.

“We are coming now.”

“...”

“Hold onto your pants and hang on!”

For a moment, something fiery surged within the hearts of the Namgung family.

“Yes!”

Clang!

Chung Myung unsheathed his sword and bared his teeth.

“Okay then...”

Everyone on the Yangtze River waited.

His next words.

“Let’s go. To save Namgung!”

Paaat!

As soon as the words were spoken, Baek Cheon and Yu Yiseol shot forward like rays of light without a moment’s hesitation. The Mount Hua and Tang families followed suit, forming a long line.

It was as if a black dragon and a green dragon were dancing across the Yangtze River.

Chung Myung kicked off the ground and leapt over the two dragons, soaring quickly into the air.

“I am coming, you bastards!”

Chung Myung’s sword, with its back to the sun rising over the Yangtze River, shone brightly, as if it would dispel all the darkness lingering over the river.

Rather than saying it was like a storm, the expression “gust of wind” might be more appropriate.

The atmosphere, once frozen like ice, suddenly turned upside down. Those who had been coldly struggling against the pirate’s advance rushed ahead, transforming into blazing flames.

“W-what is that?”

“They are coming this way!”

A scream-like shout burst out from the pirates’ mouths.

However, among those who were shouting, almost none of them actively tried to stop the intruders.

It was because they were flustered.

With Plum Blossom Island surrounded and nearly occupied, there seemed to be no need to worry about any attacks from outside. No matter how great the warriors were, it would take time to cross the distance.

During that movement, they would have to endure attacks from the ships stationed on the Yangtze River. Even if they could break through the attack, they would suffer a huge amount of damage, and even if they reached Plum Blossom Island, they would have to fight the troops occupying the island with their exhausted bodies.

Wasn’t it because they fully understood that situation that even Shaolin didn’t think of throwing themselves into the river?

Namgung Hwang had already proven with his life how disadvantageous this situation was for them. So, even if it was the recently renowned Mount Hua, they thought it would be useless.

But the response of Mount Hua surpassed their expectations entirely.

Baek Cheon’s foot kicked the wooden block. The wooden block, which had sunk slightly into the water, quickly rose again.

Paat! Paat!

Baek Cheon’s speed, stepping on the wooden blocks floating on the water like stepping stones, was no less than running on flat ground.

The distance from the riverside to Plum Blossom Island was definitely far.

But what if this place was not a river but a piece of land?

To a warrior, this distance would never be considered far.

Paaat!

The disciples of Mount Hua, clad in their black robes, lined up and sprinted after Baek Cheon, who led the way. From a distance, it appeared as if they were running on water.

Among those witnessing this unprecedented sight, someone shouted urgently.

“W-what are you all doing! You morons! Shoot! Shoot an arrow!”

Of course, their moment of inaction was so brief that it could be considered a mere instant. However, by that time, Mount Hua and the Tang family had already crossed a quarter of the river.

“SHOOOOOTTTTTT!”

Terrified, they fired arrows wildly.

They shot in haste, without properly forming a battle formation, but they were still desperate. The story behind the arrows was truly extraordinary.

The arrows soared as if they would pierce the sky and then rained down all at once, aiming for Mount Hua, who was charging ahead like the Black Dragon.

Swish!

It was as if black rain was falling from the sky.

However, Mount Hua’s swordsmen, undeterred, raised their swords towards the sky in unison.

Kakakang!

The arrows bounced cleanly off the swords, which swung with precision and without waste.

The reason bows were seldom used in murim warfare was obvious. Long-range weapons with predictable trajectories cannot penetrate martial defenses.

Nevertheless, pirates favored the bow for one reason. Those submerged in water could not block the attacks even if they saw them coming.

Yet, the rain of arrows posed no obstacle to Mount Hua’s swordsmen, who ran on the river as if it were flat ground.

“Shoot! Shoot more! Damn it, I’m telling you, slow them down!”

With their attacks ineffective, someone shouted in frustration.

Even if they couldn’t be defeated by these arrows, they had to buy time somehow.

That strategy was definitely sound.

But what he missed was that Mount Hua wasn’t the only one running on the water to save Namgung.

“Shoot faster... kuak!”

The pirates who had been shouting at the archers suddenly convulsed as if struck by lightning.

“Grrk... grrk.”

“L-leader!”

“Leader! What is...”

When the surrounding pirates turned their heads, the leader who had been giving orders was falling backward like a rotten piece of wood.

Thud!

A line of blood flowed from his forehead as he fell to the ground with his eyes rolled back.

“A-assassin dagger!”

A single sword was stuck deep into the forehead of their leader, all the way to the handle. The pirates screamed in fear.

That was the start.

“Ackkk!”

“Acccckkk!”

Those who had been lined up on the railing of the ship and shooting arrows fell down screaming, one after another, falling over the railing.

Splash! Splash!

At that moment, as no one could come to their senses due to the shocking situation, someone shouted in a fit of rage.

“Assassin weapons! Assassin weapons! Lower your body... kuak!”

Swishhh!

The Sichuan Tang family’s venom passed over the heads of those who bent down in fear. The backs of those who barely managed to escape were soaked in cold sweat.

That venom must have soaked into the Sichuan Tang family’s weapons.

With their level of skills, even just getting brushed by the needle wouldn’t guarantee them their lives. And of course, not everyone was lucky enough to avoid that spit.

“Ackkkkk!”

The one who was hit in the face by the needle that flew in fell to the ground, clutching his face.

“Ack! Accckk! Ackkkk!”

He let out a scream that was too horrible to describe and rolled on the ground, spitting up blood. Soon, his eyes rolled back, and his entire body convulsed.

“Grrk... grrrkkk!”

A dark color gradually spread from the contorted face, and soon the entire body began to be covered in red and black spots.

“Uh... ugh...”

Those watching the scene let out a groan of pain. Just looking at it made their bodies tremble.

His body, which had been convulsing as if he had epilepsy, soon collapsed. He had only been hit by a few thin needles the size of a hair, but he died before he could even catch his breath.

Those who felt the power of the poison turned pale.

It was a time of unparalleled peace. That was why everyone had forgotten.

How could the Sichuan Tang family, with its weakness in hand-to-hand combat, reach for the position of the leader of the Five Great Families?

It was because in such a battle where the sects clashed against one another, the Tang family was more dangerous than any other sect in the world.

The poisonous snake, which had been hiding in the distant land of Sichuan for a long time, finally broke its silence and began to swarm over the waters of the Yangtze River.

“Y-You bastard! Don’t just keep your heads down! Shoot arrows! Do you want to die by my hand?”

The leaders, their eyes bloodshot, swore. If they hid under the railing and survived like this, they wouldn’t be left alive. Wasn’t it obvious where the Black Dragon King’s wrath would fall?

Whether they raised their heads or lowered them, what awaited them was ultimately death.

Fortunately or unfortunately, they didn’t have to worry anymore. That’s because something other than the needle flew over their heads, which had been fully lowered.

Swisssh!

The eyes of the pirates, who had been hanging their heads low, reflexively turned up.

‘Pouch?’

The eyes of the pirates, who had discovered the small green pouch flying over their heads, quickly filled with fear.

Pung! Boom!

Several pouches that had flown in burst out in an instant, and the black poison and poisonous sand spread, covering the pirates who were crouching down.

“Eukkkkk!”

“Kuak! Ack! Ackkkk!”

Immediately, a scream so desperate that they didn’t want to hear it even in their dreams erupted from around them. The poisonous sand dug into their flesh, and the black poison covering the deck surged into their noses and mouths.

“Grrkk...”

“Kuak! Grrk!”

In an instant, the deck transformed into a field of death.

Tang Gunak, who had completely shaken a nearby ship in an instant, ordered them with cold eyes as he watched the scene.

“Save your weapons! This battle will last long!”

“Yes!”

The Tang family warriors, running behind him, answered firmly, though softly.

Meanwhile, Mount Hua continued to advance.

Chung Myung’s sharp voice rang out from the center of those running like a moving island.

“Here it comes!”

As soon as those words were spoken, Mount Hua’s swordsmen drew their swords from their sheaths. Then, they extended their swords toward the water below their feet.

Paaat!

The sword qi, emitted from the tip of the swords, pierced the water’s surface and shot downward. Even though they couldn’t clearly see the water below, there was no hesitation in their movements as they released the sword qi.

And.

“One more time!”

With Chung Myung in the sky, the swordsmen of Mount Hua once again unleashed their sword qi into the water simultaneously.

Of course, their running feet didn’t stop.

The result of the attack was immediate.

The river turned blood red.

Before the pirates, who had been targeting them from the water, could even approach the feet of Mount Hua’s swordsmen, they were pierced by the sword qi and died.

Paat!

Some of Mount Hua’s disciples kicked the wooden block and jumped up. At the same time, a long harpoon shot out of the water, piercing the wooden block they were stepping on.

Paat!

A bright blue sword qi flew in, and before the pirates who had stabbed the harpoon could even react, their bodies were slashed.

The pirate clutching the harpoon, unable to even scream properly, trembled and convulsed, while the disciples of Mount Hua charged forward without pausing to check if he was alive or dead.

Paat!

Dozens of pirates rose up in front of Baek Cheon and Yu Yiseol, attempting to block their path.

However, Baek Cheon and Yu Yiseol didn’t slow down; instead, they increased their speed.

Tat!

Yu Yiseol lightly kicked off a wooden block and climbed onto Baek Cheon’s shoulder. Baek Cheon, anticipating her move, tilted his head slightly to provide a foothold for her.

And!

Taat!

Yu Yiseol, stepping off Baek Cheon’s shoulder, soared into the air like a hawk. Before she could touch the water, she began to fall behind the pirates hovering in the air.

Baek Cheon followed suit and pressed ahead.

“Haaaah!”

Baek Cheon’s sword unleashed dozens of forms, enveloping the pirates in front of him all at once.

“What?”

The pirates’ eyes widened in shock. It was an unbelievable display of sword qi from just one person’s sword.

However, their surprise was premature. Before the sword qi could reach their bodies, Yu Yiseol’s sword, which had flown behind them, also emitted dozens of sword qi.

A storm of sword qi flew in from both the front and the back simultaneously, achieved by just the two of them against dozens of pirates.

The result was devastating.

’Ackkkk!“

“Acccck!”

The sword qi, vigorous and relentless, pierced the bodies of the pirates who had leaped up.

Swish!

As Baek Cheon’s feet pushed against the water surface, his sword slashed horizontally once more.

Paaang!

With a sound like a long whip being swung, the bodies of the pirates, pierced by the sword qi, were slashed horizontally and quickly thrown aside.

“I will make the way!”

Baek Cheon’s roar, as if piercing the sky, burst from his mouth.

“Run! Save the Namgung family!”

“Yes!”

The Mount Hua swordsmen ran like lightning over the blood-soaked river. Plum Blossom Island was getting closer and closer.

Arrows rained down relentlessly, sharp harpoons flying continuously from beneath the water.

The place where all of this must converge was none other than the Yangtze River.

The situation was so dire that even the phrase ‘bad conditions’ seemed inadequate, yet the feet of the Mount Hua swordsmen never paused for even a moment.

The boiling blood of the one leading the charge seemed to energize those following behind.

And Namgung Do-Wei, running alongside in this intense flow, was determined not to fall behind.

‘This is Mount Hua.’

The one who understood best how difficult and terrifying it was to confront the pirates on the Yangtze River was Namgung Do-Wei. The terrible wounds on his body were proof of his experiences.

However, the Mount Hua swordsmen were running as if this place were flat ground, swinging their swords even in the obviously difficult and frightening situation.

Was it thanks to the wooden blocks?

‘As if!’

Clank!

At that moment, he accidentally stepped on a wooden block to the side and slipped, but Jo Gul grabbed him and pulled him back up.

“T-thank you.”

“Don’t worry about it and just run ahead! I am here to help!”

“Yes!”

It might seem easy.

But it was definitely not easy to run while stepping precisely on small wooden blocks floating on the flowing river. So, it was not just about running, but maintaining speed! It was definitely something beyond shocking.

If they hadn’t protected him, Namgung Do-Wei would have fallen into the water several times already. However, they were holding Namgung Do-Wei and running while deflecting the pouring arrows and repelling the pirates attacking them.

How was this possible? Just how?

Their skill was awe-inspiring. It was as if they had fought countless fierce battles in such environments and had survived many life-threatening situations in places where normal warriors wouldn’t dare to tread.

Indeed, one could say they were exceptionally skilled.

It was impossible, but they seemed accustomed to such a fighting style. What kind of training did they usually do that would allow people of a similar age to him to show such behavior?

Did he not know the sect called Mount Hua as well as he thought? Or did the world not realize the sect called Mount Hua?

Just by what they have shown so far, the world’s perception of them as a common sword sect of Shaanxi was completely shattered.

‘No! No!’

Namgung Do-Wei, who had been thinking, bit his lip until it bled.

This part alone shouldn’t be evaluated. Even if everyone in the world paid attention to their skill, Namgung Do-Wei had to see something different.

They knew. The moment they jumped into the river, what kind of battle would they have to fight?

This is why what Namgung Do-Wei had to see was not their strength, but their courage.

The courage to jump into the rain of arrows without hesitation.

The courage to be dragged into the dark water and run into the river, where the pirates swarmed around.

Even if the whole world doesn’t know, Namgung Do-Wei must understand their courage.

And so...

‘Even if I have to die, I will not be a burden for you!’

“Tat!”

Namgung Do-Wei fiercely swung his sword and struck down the arrows. Chung Myung, who saw that, smiled even under the pouring arrows.

“Nice one!”

Namgung Do-Wei looked at Chung Myung. Chung Myung smiled and said,

“Follow me with the determination to die!”

“Yes!”

Paaat!

Chung Myung’s sword flew in like a ray of light and accurately cut off the necks of the pirates rising from under the water.

At the same time, his gaze turned to the back. He glanced at the Tang family and shouted without hesitation.

“Jo Gul sahyung!”

“Okay!”

No further words were needed.

Namgung Do-Wei couldn’t understand at all, but it seemed like that was alone enough to convey the meaning to him.

Jo Gul, who was running as if escorting Namgung Do-Wei, kicked off a block and leaped up. Then, like a bird targeting a fish, he shot powerfully into the water.

Splash!

Jo Gul plunged into the water with a splash, resembling a cannonball, and simultaneously, some of the Mount Hua swordsmen sprinting at full speed followed him in.

Without clear instructions, some jumped into the water while others continued running without even glancing back.

‘What, what is that?’

A moment of confusion appeared on Namgung Do-Wei’s face.

Meanwhile, deep in the waters of the Yangtze River.

Oh Cha-hyung quickly ascended towards the surface. Surrounding him were the members of the Black Shark Pirates that he led.

‘Damn it!’

The first unit that attacked Mount Hua had become fish food. They were pierced by the sword qi shooting into the water without even managing a proper harpoon strike. And that wasn’t all. Those who barely managed to surface had their heads cut off without even taking down one opponent.

These people attacked and defended as if they could see right through the water. The reason his body temperature gradually dropped wasn’t simply due to the cold water.

But there was no need to target the difficult enemies. There was more than just the Mount Hua sect there.

‘Tang family morons!’

In a way, the pirates, who had mastered the water arts, were natural enemies of the Sichuan Tang family. Any assassin’s weapon would slow down in water, and no poison would work effectively.

There was no need to fight them in the water. If they could harpoon just one from below and drag them into the depths, the Tang family warriors would be doomed.

As the Tang family finally reached above him, Oh Cha-Hyung’s eyes flashed. When he signaled to attack, the Black Sharks doubled their speed, rising towards the surface like arrows.

‘I will make you regret coming into this river!’

But at that very moment—

“Grrrk!”

A scream echoed even underwater. Oh Cha-Hyung instinctively turned his head and saw one of the Black Shark members foaming at the mouth and trembling.

‘Why?’

No, it wasn’t just foam that was coming out.

The blood flowing from the side dyed the river red.

‘W-when?’

When on earth did he get attacked?

The question was resolved soon.

Oh Cha-Hyung stared blankly at the unbelievable sight, his eyes wide open.

Behind his subordinates, who were sinking into the water, the Mount Hua warriors in black were rushing towards them with astonishing speed.

Mount Hua was a mountain in Shaanxi. In other words, they had no connection with water in their entire lives.

But the movements of those he was seeing before his eyes were so fast that it was hard to believe they hadn’t been trained in water arts.

‘How...’

Swish!

And at that moment, the sword of the young man charging ahead split the water like a ray of light. The unbelievable swiftness of the sword, which was hard to imagine in the water, swung and pierced the throat of Oh Cha-Hyung in an instant.

“Grr... grrk...”

Along with the pain that overwhelmed him, the river water rushed into the hole in his nose. The feeling of heading towards death was more disgusting and vivid than he could have imagined.

Crack!

The sword twisted inside his neck. It was pulled out after tearing open the wound and leaving an unhealed mark.

“Grrrk....”

Oh Cha-Hyung’s body slowly began to sink deep into the water.

The last thing Oh Cha-Hyung saw in his fading consciousness was the proud smile hanging on the lips of the young swordsman who was passing by above him.

‘Mount Hua...’

The true value of the training they had done in the valley of Mount Hua, swinging the sword as if their lives depended on it, was displayed on the Yangtze River. Compared to the deep, swirling valley, the wide waves of the Yangtze were nothing to them.

Led by Jo Gul, the swordsmen guarded the lower side of the Tang family and swung their sharp swords at the pirates.

“Run!”

Baek Cheon charged ahead.

Rather than cutting down the pirates who occasionally stabbed at him with harpoons one by one, he focused on avoiding and jumping over them without slowing down. He knew that when a group of this size runs in a line, if the leader’s speed slows down, everyone will get entangled.

And no matter what happened, he could never stop. The lives of the people of the Namgung family and his sajaes were at his feet now!

That was the moment.

Kwaaaang!

With a deafening roar, something flew towards Baek Cheon at an incredible speed.

‘White Thunder Cannon?’

Baek Cheon could clearly see the shells flying through the air. But before he could even make a choice, someone jumped and moved towards the shells.

Kwaaang!

The one who rose to the shell swung the sword and dodged the incoming shells.

“Yoon Jong!”

The moment Baek Cheon shouted his name instinctively, Baek Sang flew to another shell.

Kwaaang!

Baek Sang’s sword slashed down another shell!

“Keep going, sahyung!”

“Do not stop!”

“How dare you give me orders, morons!”

Baek Cheon grinned and ran ahead.

Kwang! Kwang!

But the pirates were not going to give up so easily. The approaching pirate ship turned and aimed at them. The muzzle of the cannon on the deck kept firing.

“Fuck..!”

Just as anger flashed across Baek Cheon’s face.

“Sasuk!”

A voice came.

No further words were needed.

Baek Cheon instinctively twisted his waist and took the stance to swing the sword, but not with the blade, but rather with the blade’s body!

Paat!

Then, something flew from behind like a flying squirrel and placed a heavy weight onto the sword. Before the sensation could even reach his head, Baek Cheon swung the sword with all his might and sent the object on top of the sword flying far away.

Swiiish!

A single ray of black light flew across the waters of the Yangtze River.

“W-what is that?”

The pirates were terrified as they saw something flying toward them like a cannonball.

Kwaaaang!

The human cannonball that Baek Cheon launched soon landed like a meteor on the deck of the ship, which had been firing shells.

The solid wooden deck shattered like tofu, and the splintered wood flew in all directions.

Before the flying pieces of wood could even hit the floor, an eerie black aura cut through the pirates on the deck.

Slash! Slash!

Red blood splashed on the wood, shooting up into the sky. The blood of the pirates left dark stains on Chung Myung’s face as he cut them down.

“Ah... Ahhh....”

Paat!

Chung Myung, having brushed the blood off his sword blade, turned his gaze to the pirates who met his eyes. The moment their eyes met, Chung Myung’s lips twisted, revealing his pure white teeth.

“This...”

His voice seemed to carry the scent of blood.

“This is the fight you started.”

Kwaang!

Chung Myung, having kicked off the deck, charged into the midst of the pirates. Dozens of heads rose into the air, and blood gushed all around the ship and into the Yangtze River.

Mount Hua’s Demon.

It was the moment when Mount Hua’s Demon, who had terrified the demons of the Demonic Cult, appeared on the Yangtze River.

The center of the scattered blood drops was cut with eerie precision by a sword sharpened to an uncanny degree. The sword, which drew a vivid line, severed the neck of the man whose mouth was agape.

Puung!

It was a perfect cut that did not even register the slightest resistance at the tip of the blade.

The hearts of the pirates who witnessed this sight were naturally filled with fear.

The Justice faction sects would never sympathize with their feelings upon seeing this sword. Only those who had experienced it could understand it.

Those who had killed countless people knew it: it was absolutely not easy for a person to kill another.

No matter how accustomed one was to killing, there was bound to be a small hesitation when stabbing a person in a vital spot. That was a human instinct that could not be overcome by will or determination.

However, there was no such hesitation in this man’s sword.

Even at the moment of slicing down another human, there was no hesitation or wavering.

A sword that could kill countless people or a sword that could be used without pause.

The pirates, who knew from experience what kind of beings those who wielded such swords were, could not help but tremble with a cold feeling.

‘Ugh...’

So maybe it was fortunate.

The fact that the demon didn’t even give them a chance to properly feel that genuine fear.

Kwang!

The deck burst open as Chung Myung charged towards the pirates.

“Ughhhh!”

The terrified ones screamed and hurled their spears as if in a fit of panic.

It wasn’t a stab meant to knock down the opponent. It was a movement closer to an instinct to block the opponent’s approach, not a will to kill.

Regardless of their intentions, the sight of dozens of harpoons flying at one person must have been terrifying.

However, the young swordsman with a cold chill in his eyes simply smiled with one corner of his lips raised, watching the dozens of harpoons flying towards him.

Sssh.

It was a sight like something out of a fantasy book.

You could step back. Counter.

There must be dozens of ways to respond to the opponent’s attack. However, the method Chung Myung chose was the most reckless and efficient.

Shhh.

Chung Myung slipped into the small gap revealed between the dozens of harpoons like a ghost.

Kakakng!

He lightly knocked away the harpoons coming from the side and twisted his shoulder to push away the ones that had passed by. That is how he carved his own space through the forest of harpoons.

The pirates opened their eyes wide.

At that moment, they encountered a single devil laughing whitely among the countless harpoons.

Pffft.

The sword aimed at the lower left moved slightly ahead.

The sword strike that started slowly accelerated explosively and drew a perfectly straight line.

In an instant, the red sword slash that cut through the pirates’ necks flew through the face of another pirate. The pirates let out animal-like howls without even thinking about running.

Paaat!

No, he tried to puke. But before that could happen, Chung Myung cut off his neck.

In an instant, five bodies were split in half. Their upper bodies, unable to handle the power of the sword, were cut off and shot into the air, spinning like tops.

The slashed blood poured down like rain from the sky over the Yangtze River.

A single sword killed more than a dozen people. The pirates stepped back hesitantly, watching the blood splash from the bodies of those who hadn’t yet fallen.

There were still dozens of comrades for them. All there was was one enemy.

But what did all those facts even mean now?

Chung Myung turned his gaze as he was hit by the slashed blood.

His face was still wet with blood that hadn’t yet cooled down, and his black hair was stuck to it.

The dark eyes stared at the pirates without any emotion.

“Ugh, uhh...”

Swish!

Chung Myung, who had finally brushed off the blood on his sword, moved as if looking for his next prey.

“Uckkkkkk!”

“S-save me!”

The collapse began.

The pirates screamed desperately and turned around. Unaware that their harpoons would pierce their comrades’ bodies, they grabbed those running in front of them and started fleeing at a speed they had never experienced before.

There was only one thing on their minds.

‘I WANT TO LIVE!’

Being in the same space as that devil meant death for all. For the pirates who understood this fact, their sole task on earth was to escape the devil’s clutches and survive.

The thought of being punished for running away was already out of their minds. How could those who had learned what true fear was, have the luxury of thinking about anything else?

Those whose minds were blank threw themselves off the ship, screaming. If they just jumped into the river, that devil wouldn’t chase after them anymore.

But unfortunately, the devil had no mercy once it got a taste of blood.

Paat!

The sword moved without a moment’s hesitation, slicing through the pirates’ backs. It struck down the ankles of those who kicked the deck and severed the spines of those who had fallen.

Boom!

Chung Myung, who had kicked off the floor again, shot up and drew out sword qi one after another towards the backs of the pirates who were jumping into the river.

The crescent-shaped sword qi slashed at those who dared turn their backs on him, cutting them down without hesitation.

“Accck! Euk!”

“Kuaaaak!”

The screams of the pirates pierced by the sword qi in the air poured down miserably onto the waters of the Yangtze River.

Plop! Plop!

The pirates who had reached the water they so desperately wanted, where the devil’s sword couldn’t reach, were unfortunately unable to rejoice. The dead cannot rejoice or be sad.

Tak!

Chung Myung’s gaze, which had landed on the deck, turned to the trembling pirate in front of him. He was someone whose ankle had been cut off and couldn’t jump into the water. His face turned pale, and he stumbled.

“H-how can you, towards someone running....”

Then Chung Myung laughed.

“If you fight each other to kill each other and then turn your back and run, do you expect mercy?”

Chung Myung slowly approached him.

“That...”

“You people do whatever you want, but we have to show mercy? Because we are the Justice faction?”

As he approached, the pirate’s eyes trembled.

“Of course we can. There are people like that. But...”

Stab!

Chung Myung’s sword mercilessly pierced the pirate’s heart.

“But not me.”

The pirate stared blankly at his chest where the sword was stabbed.

Paat!

The moment the sword was pulled out, his head fell down as if he died. Without any more hesitation, Chung Myung looked up and confirmed that there was no one standing on the deck. He kicked the ship ahead without hesitation and pushed himself into the air.

Kwaang!

He then kicked the water and moved ahead, twisting the corners of his mouth.

Where he was headed was not to the main force of Mount Hua but to another ship approaching Mount Hua.

“T-these damned bastards!”

A roar erupted from the Black Dragon’s mouth.

“What the hell are you doing! You damned bastards!”

His eyes were bloodshot.

Those damned Mount Hua people were now freely crossing the Yangtze River, but the pirates he boasted about were unable to stop them at all.

Neither the arrows he poured down nor the spears they threw stabbed anyone. Even the White Thunder Cannons he had placed were being taken down in an instant.

There was no way to understand how that could even happen.

The Namgung family and even Shaolin were afraid of the pirates because the Yangtze River was their playground. So how was Mount Hua fighting while overwhelming them on the waters?

“Floating platform!”

The Black Dragon King roared until his throat tore.

“Destroy that platform, you morons! Don’t just fight back—destroy the platform! If you just drag them into the water, they are nothing! Aim for the damned platform in front of them!”

Clearly, that judgment was correct.

Sometimes, very small factors can make a huge difference. It was clear that this entire situation stemmed from the small wooden blocks the Mount Hua disciples were stepping on.

However, any judgment is meaningless if it isn’t made quickly.

The Plum Blossom Island, which once seemed too far to cross, was now close enough to reach in an instant if you ran on flat ground. As the Black Dragon King shouted, the leader of Mount Hua’s rally almost reached the island.

“Those fucking rats!”

The Black Dragon King raised the crescent moon blade with a determination to destroy the ship with his attack.

Wooong!

At the end of the sword, an incredible qi began to form. His red eyes glared at Baek Cheon, who was leading Mount Hua.

“DIEEEEEEE!”

Kwaaaak!

His blade swung down fiercely from above!

The sword qi exploded towards Baek Cheon. It was the very force that made the Emperor’s Sword sleep eternally in the flowing water. It was the sword qi that clearly proved why the name of the Black Dragon King reigned in fear over the Yangtze River.

Kwaaaa!

The sword qi tore through the air, and the qi wave alone drew a long line on the river.

The Black Dragon King was certain that this blade qi could change the situation. The flimsy sword could never block this.

At that moment, Baek Cheon, who was running in a straight line, caught a glimpse of the flying blade qi. But that was it.

Baek Cheon clearly saw the attack but looked forward again and ran faster.

It was the moment the Black Dragon King inhaled.

“Am-mi-ta-bha!”

A roar came.

A roar that could be heard anywhere in the Central Plains, but a roar that couldn’t be heard there, on the Yangtze River.

“What?”

Soon, someone rose like a ray of light in front of the blade qi, which was flying towards Baek Cheon. A golden, magnificent Buddhist light emanating from him poured out like the sun.

“S-Shaolin?”

The Black Dragon King was shocked.

“AHHHH!”

Hae Yeon, who had risen up, thrust his fist forward with all his might.

Wooong!

The Hundred Steps Divine Fist he unleashed collided with the flying blade qi.

The blade qi, which had been flying straight towards Baek Cheon, twisted upwards, passed over the Mount Hua disciples, and crashed into the ship encircling them from the other side.

Kwaaang!

“Ackkkk!”

“Water is coming in! Stop it! The ship is sinking!”

The power of the blade qi was amazing, splitting the bottom of the ship entirely. The blue water of the Yangtze River surged into it like a whirlpool.

However, the sight of the ship did not even enter the vision of the Black Dragon King.

His gaze was fixed on the one person who managed to deflect the blade qi.

The man was wearing a black robe like the disciples of Mount Hua. But even so, wasn’t his identity obvious?

There was just one question.

“Why is Shaolin here? Why!”

But there was no one there to answer his shout.

Meanwhile, Baek Cheon, who believed in Hae Yeon and ignored the Black Dragon King’s blade, pushed forward even more and ran ahead.

Kwang!

After a while, Baek Cheon’s body, which had been kicking the wooden block as if it would shatter, rose into the air. He leaped over the river, which was overflowing, and became a black line cutting across the blue sky.

And finally.

Thud!

Baek Cheon’s two feet landed on the ground, not on the wooden block.

Right there, on Plum Blossom Island.

“...”

The pirates he encountered at the edge of the island looked at him with tired faces.

Baek Cheon, who had bent down to land on the ground, slowly raised his upper body and opened his mouth.

“Follow the sect leader’s orders.”

“...”

“Defeat the pirates and rescue the Namgung family.”

Baek Cheon raised his sword and aimed ahead.

“GOOO!”

At that moment.

The Mount Hua swordsmen who had arrived at Plum Blossom Island then passed Baek Cheon and rushed towards the pirates.

“Ahhhhh!”

“Defeat them!”

A red sword qi rose from the tips of their swords at once. The sword qi soon bloomed brilliantly, creating red plum blossoms worthy of the name on one side of the Plum Blossom Sword.

Truly beautiful and intense.

“S-stop them! Stop!”

The black-robed swordsmen surged forward in unison, lowering their stances.

A deep red plum blossom seemed to bloom among them.

This overwhelming sight struck fear even into those unfamiliar with the name of Mount Hua. A chilling aura, sharp as a blade, enveloped the pirates.

“U-ughhh!”

The ones at the front swung their weapons wildly. Despite the panic, the pirates’ harpoons, renowned for their swiftness and sharpness, lived up to their reputation.

Yet, before their attacks could fully land, a sword would flash like lightning, striking them down.

Chaaang!

A sharp metallic sound pierced the air as the harpoon was knocked skyward.

‘Kuak!’

Just as the pirate gritted his teeth, attempting to retrieve the harpoon swiftly—

Swish!

A sword darted in from behind like a ray of light.

‘What is this?’

By the time he realized, it was already too late. His eyes widened in shock.

Puak! Puak! Puak!

In an instant, three sword blades pierced his body.

“Grrk...”

The pirate’s body trembled. At that moment, pain was overshadowed by the question of ‘how?’

Of course, it was natural to coordinate with one’s comrades. Didn’t they also strive to attack efficiently by observing the changing surroundings?

But this was beyond that level.

The moment the swordsman in front raised his weapon, a sword was already flying in from behind. Suppose the movements of the one in front had deviated even slightly from expectation. In that case, the sword might have struck their comrades, too.

How could they attack with such precision? What did they even believe in?

‘Insane... bastards...’

His thoughts couldn’t continue.

Paaat!

A sword slashed deeply into his chest. Without sparing a glance at the fallen pirate, the swordsmen of Mount Hua pressed forward.

Paaat!

Paaat!

The swords sliced through the air, one after another.

There were no flashy changes. No dazzling swords moving around. The swords swung by the swordsmen of Mount Hua moved without any waste, only to cut off the pirates’ breath.

The Mount Hua disciples, with their expressionless, hardened faces and extremely efficient sword techniques, were surprisingly pressuring the pirates.

“Block them! Clothe them all! There are just a few of them! Block the remaining ones here so they cannot land anymore!”

As the pirates who had occupied Plum Blossom Island retreated hesitantly, one of the pirate elders shouted out.

“Push them with the water and drive them to the edge of the place! Do not give them ground to step on!”

In theory, it might have been a good strategy to implement. But wasn’t a strategy only meaningful when it was carried out perfectly to the end?

“Samae!”

“Yes.”

Tat. Tat. Tatatatatar!

Before Baek Cheon could finish speaking, Yu Yiseol began to move. The sound of her kicking her feet turned faster and faster, and soon, she turned into lightning and rushed in front of the Mount Hua line.

“Eik!”

“Die!”

The pirates who saw her flying like a ghost were startled and swung their harpoons. No, they tried to swing their harpoons.

At that moment.

Paat!

Yu Yiseol accelerated twice as fast as she had initially moved and arrived right in front of the pirates who had pulled their harpoons back to attack.

No, from the pirates’ perspective, it was a dramatic movement that was more appropriately described as ‘appeared’ rather than ‘arrived’.

And her core was faster than their feet.

Before the pirates could even show their surprise, the sword cut through the space and cut them all down in one go.

Ptttt!

Blood splashed out like a fountain from the wounds. The entire front row collapsed in an instant.

“Cover!”

But, as if to prove they had been through countless battles, the pirates immediately stepped on the backs of their fallen comrades and leaped to attack Yu Yiseol.

“No can do.”

At that moment, a man rose from behind Yu Yiseol, creating dozens of sword forms in the air, sweeping away the pirates.

“Acccck!”

“Accck!”

The pirates screamed and were thrown back as they were struck by the sword qi that appeared out of thin air.

Yoon Jong, who had crushed the pirates targeting Yu Yiseol with a single sword, kicked the ground as soon as he landed, rushing to assist the sago, who was already running ahead.

Yu Yiseol straightened her body and charged into the pirates, beginning to swing her sword as if dancing.

Swisssh!

The sword, swinging gracefully in the air, pierced the pirates’ bodies here and there.

Sash! Slash! Slash!

The one whose thigh was cut screamed and fell, and the one whose neck was cut held his neck and collapsed. However, their situation was far better than those whose hearts were torn apart by a single sword.

“D-Die, you wench!”

A harpoon filled with qi exploded and hurled towards Yu Yiseol’s head. Instead of retreating, she took another step towards the harpoon.

And then, an amazingly swift sword!

Slash!

Her sword cut the wrist of the pirate holding the harpoon.

Slash!

Her sword, which immediately rose up in reverse as if twisting, cut off his elbow as well.

And then.

Kwaang!

The moment her sword finally struck the harpoon, the harpoon, filled with qi, lost its direction and flew towards another harpoon aimed at Yu Yiseol.

Crack! Crack!

The harpoon, which easily pierced a person’s body without losing its force, continued to stab the person behind. The pirates, hit by the harpoon and skewered, collapsed with eyes filled with shock and disbelief.

In the blink of an eye, three sword strikes completely crushed their tactics.

Her movements didn’t stop for a moment.

Slash.

Red blood gushed out from the throat of the pirate who was hit.

Yu Yiseol, who had cut off the head cleanly, lowered her form as if she were going to crouch. She bent down so that her body almost touched the ground, spun around like a top, and successively split open the knees of the pirates rushing at her.

“Ack! Ackkkk!”

“M-My legs! My legs!”

Swish!

Yu Yiseol, spinning around as if sweeping the floor, bounced up and swung her sword in three swift attacks. She pushed the hesitant pirates back, glanced ahead with an indifferent look, and flew to the side like a swallow skimming across the water.

At the same time, a black shadow flew to where her body had been.

Paaat!

Soon, this sharp sword, which was beyond comparison to ‘lightning,’ pierced the necks of the pirates in front.

Slash!

The sharp metal blade cut through the soft human flesh, and the sound of bones cracking followed. Before the sound could fully resonate, the sword, which had been retrieved, pursued those who had retreated.

Cold eyes, firmly shut lips.

Tang Soso’s expression was now difficult to describe with any one word other than belonging to a real swordsman. Her sword swelled in an instant and swept ahead.

At that moment, the pirates had to feel the opposite. What kind of sight must those fighting pirates have seen? Dozens of sword forms shot out from the tip of her sword as if dozens of enemies were cutting them down.

“Accck!”

Those who couldn’t dodge were helplessly swept by the sword qi. The pirates, whose bodies were riddled with holes the size of a child’s foot, collapsed on the spot, bleeding.

Yu Yiseol, Yoon Jong, Tang Soso.

Those three created a clear crack in the dense, forest-like formation of the pirates.

“Push ahead!”

The swordsmen of Mount Hua rushed into the cracks the three had made as if it were a given.

The pirates, still reeling from the shock of the sword form and sword qi, were brutally attacked by the Mount Hua swordsmen who had rushed in.

A desperate scream of fear filled the air over Plum Blossom Island.

The swordsmen ran without changing their expressions, focused solely on taking the lives of the pirates. The pirates, completely overwhelmed by their momentum, instinctively kept retreating.

“S-stop them! You damn bastards! Do not back down and stop them!”

The elder giving orders turned pale.

They had already proven their skill against the Namgung family. Wasn’t suppressing the opponent by pushing a group into a limited space one of the pirates’ strengths?

But such tactics were not working on this sect at all.

Because they were strong?

Nonsense!

They had already fought against the Emperor’s Sword of the Namgung family. No matter how strong these young warriors were, could they be as strong as them?

But the scene unfolding before the elder’s eyes now was so different from when he had fought the Namgung family.

He couldn’t even believe the sight, but he understood the cause of all this more clearly.

‘What on earth did they do to be like this?’

They didn’t seem to be fighting like the Justice faction sects at all.

It was ridiculous, but these bastards were used to fighting. They knew how to fight when groups of people clashed at once.

Perhaps even more than the pirates, who were known to fight countless wars.

They looked like worn-out veteran warriors who had fought a hundred wars.

What was even more terrifying was that there was no hesitation in the swords of those bastards. They did not even have a single hair on their faces to show their manliness, yet the hands moving the swords showed no hesitation in taking lives.

What else could you call those insane warriors if not Sword Demons?

“You morons! Don’t try to kill them; just hold your ground! Just once! Just once, stop their force!”

In a fight between a few, skill was the deciding factor, but in a fight between many, morale and momentum were the deciding factors. If they could stop their feet just once, it wouldn’t be impossible to crush them later with the advantage of numbers.

“Get in line! Use the corpses of those in front of you as shields to hold on! They will definitely get exhausted!”

If that lofty momentum was broken once, Mount Hua would also showcase its weakness of being short in number. So, just once, they had to tie their feet.

The pirates who heard the elder’s order gritted their teeth and pressed their shoulders against their comrades. They planned to defend as firmly as possible and first defend where they stood.

But their misfortune was... that Mount Hua wasn’t the only one who had set foot on Plum Blossom Island.

Swish! Swish!

“What?”

“That is...?”

Green pouches flew one after another over the heads of the pirates who had braced their formation to increase their defense.

And after a while.

Pung! Boom!

The black poison that gushed out as the pouches burst covered the heads of the densely packed pirates.

“P-Poison!”

“Ahhh! Fucking bastards!”

“Cough! Cough! My b-breath...!”

Finally, Tang Gunak, who had stepped onto the ground of Plum Blossom, gritted his teeth and let out a roar.

“It is time to take revenge for the Namgung family! Do not let a single one of them off this island alive!”

“Yes!”

The warriors of the Sichuan Tang family followed the Mount Hua sect. The pouches that came out of their sleeves burst one after another over the heads of the pirates who had just remade their formation, throwing out poison.

They were so densely packed together that they had nowhere to retreat or go, and they couldn’t even think of evading, so they were hit by the poison and grabbed their throats.

“Grrrrk!”

Blood was bubbling out of their mouths. Some were rolling their eyes back and collapsing, while others madly scratched their throats until they bled.

It was the very essence of the poison.

The moment the Tang family confronted the pirates, a man struck from the forefront of Mount Hua.

KWAAANG!

Baek Cheon cut down and blew away five pirates with his sword, accurately capturing the attention of the Namgung family survivors beyond the pirates.

“I will open the way! Follow me with the determination to die!”

“Yes!”

Baek Cheon, furious at the situation, charged ahead. Mount Hua’s swordsmen and Tang family’s warriors followed him with high spirits.

Mount Hua and the Tang family.

The two sects, which were the pillars of the Heavenly Friends Alliance, first revealed their power to the world on the Yangtze River.

For anyone living in the murim world, anyone who had ever held the ideals of Harmony and Justice in their hearts, this was a sight that would make their hearts tremble and shiver in awe.

But across the river, there was a man who couldn’t help but tremble at this sight. The entire body of this man shook.

“... how...”

The eyes of Shaolin’s abbot were filled with shock and disbelief.

Finally, the Tang family had also stepped onto Plum Blossom Island.

The fingertips of the abbot, who had been watching this with wide eyes, trembled.

‘This... what is this?’

That was insane. Using the wooden blocks as stepping stones to cross the river?

‘That is easy to say!’

This path could be cut off at any time. If the pirates had just responded a little faster and destroyed those blocks in front and behind when they reached the middle of the river, Mount Hua and the Tang family would have been isolated on the blocks, which were no better than a single-leaf ship.

What kind of disaster would have happened after that?

This could not be called a strategy. Something that could not be discussed later wasn’t even worth calling a strategy.

A strategy was to at least inflict some proper damage on the opponent and then get killed. But that was nothing more than an insane act, which would not cause much damage to the opponent and would just send them to hell if even one step of the plan was off.

But...

“How did that thing work!”

The abbot’s voice burst out, mixed with shock, anger, and resentment.

It was ridiculous. Why was that reckless method being used! Without any price to be paid!

It was like watching those who jumped into the fit of hell’s fire with oil on their backs and walked through the fire with no burns.

Common sense would suggest that those who clicked their tongues at the sight would be right, but when things turned out as such, don’t all those who were watching the sight turn fools?

That was exactly what Shaolin was like now.

No matter how many times he thought about it, his decision was right. Jumping into the Yangtze River, guarded by those pirates and the Ten Thousand People Clan, was no different from committing suicide.

But what did that mean now?

Even though the odds of safely arriving at Plum Blossom Island were as slim as finding a needle in a haystack, since the Heavenly Friends Alliance actually succeeded, Shaolin, who had been hesitating to act, would end up looking foolish.

“This...!”

The abbot couldn’t contain his anger, shaking his fist and glaring across the river.

Those who only looked at the surface would say that Mount Hua and the Tang family succeeded by exploiting the pirates’ weaknesses, but if one looked deeper, that wasn’t the case.

There was only one reason why all this happened.

“Jang... Ilso....”

The abbot clenched his teeth.

Jang Ilso, who was as cunning as the devil when it came to strategy, couldn’t have overlooked what the abbot had in mind. If it were him, he would have leisurely waited for Mount Hua and the Tang family to reach the center of the river and then dismantled the wooden blocks ahead and behind.

But Jang Ilso didn’t make a move.

No, it wasn’t that he didn’t move.

The Ten Thousand People clan’s ship was on the other side of Plum Blossom Island, to the south, surrounding the island. That was a position that couldn’t threaten Mount Hua and the Tang family.

On the surface, it seemed like he was surrounding Plum Blossom Island and pressuring the Namgung family, but in reality, he was just sitting back and observing everything happening in the Yangtze River.

As if he wanted Mount Hua and the Tang family to rescue the Namgung family safely and return!

“Jang Ilso! You fucking son of a bitch!”

A desperate curse escaped from the abbot’s mouth.

The vicious malice made his body tremble. What was even more painful was that no one in the world would believe what he was witnessing.

Who would believe it?

Even if he spoke kindly and shouted that this was entirely Jang Ilso’s plan, no one would listen to the abbot.

They would just laugh at him, dismissing it as Shaolin’s struggle in a crisis.

This place was a dead end. There was nowhere else to retreat.

“Abbot...”

The abbot turned with a stern face. Jong Ri-Hyung, who had been watching the situation, asked in a low voice.

“... shouldn’t we at least do something?”

“...”

“... it would have been better if they didn’t come, but if we just watch and leave this place, the world’s criticism directed towards us will not be low. Shouldn’t we do something now?”

But what? What exactly could be done?

Were they supposed to follow the path Mount Hua and the Tang family made and show off? Are they supposed to chase off the Heavenly Friends Alliance despite being one of the Nine Great Sects?

Jong Ri-Hyung, who was observing the abbot’s face, added with an anxious look.

“Of course, I know it is a difficult decision to make. But rather than just watching, it would be better to...”

“Wait.”

“Abbot!”

“Didn’t I tell you to wait!”

Jong Ri-Hyung’s face went stiff in a moment of shock. His eyes showed disappointment in the abbot.

But the abbot could only bite his lips.

‘No.’

He could not do this.

It was hard to explain. No, no matter how much he could explain, a person like Jong Ri-Hyung would not understand.

What would even happen if they jumped into the water now?

The vicious Jang Ilso would ignore Mount Hua and the Tang family and come after Shaolin alone.

To spread the word to the world that Mount Hua and the Tang family had done it without much difficulty, but Shaolin suffered enormous damage by chasing after them.

The abbot could see it happening.

That vicious and evil trap that Jang Ilso had laid out on the Yangtze River.

‘Damn it.’

At least if there was the Gentle Crow Beggar, he would have understood why he couldn’t move and defended the abbot!

Of all places, the Gentle Crow Beggar attached himself to Mount Hua. He must be seeing with his own eyes everything that Mount Hua was accomplishing among those advancing with their swords.

“Ugh...”

His heart felt like it was set ablaze.

It seemed as if everything that happened on this Yangtze River had been arranged to trap them, Shaolin.

“Then why not just head back?”

At that moment, a sarcastic voice suddenly cut through the air.

It was a voice so cold that it was hard to believe it was directed at the abbot.

When the abbot reflexively turned his head, Hae Bang was looking at him with a sneer.

“If you are just going to watch without stepping forward, then why should we stay here?”

“... Hae Bang.”

“Or are you telling me to watch and learn from this? The heroic deeds of Mount Hua? Or the sight of Hae Yeon, dressed in black robes, saving the Namgung? Are you telling me to wear the black robe of Mount Hua instead of Shaolin’s yellow ones to act as I please?”

“Can you shut up, you fool!”

Beop Gye shouted, unable to listen any longer.

But his voice also lost some of its authority. It seemed as if he knew that no matter how severely he accused them now, his words wouldn’t work on the disciples.

Hae Bang stared at the abbot and Beop Gye and said,

“There is something that the elder once told me.”

His eyes were colder than ever.

“Pursuing one’s own Buddhist law is not the only way. A true Buddhist should know how to lead the poor people to Buddhism, even if it means burning our own body. It is hypocrisy and a sin to just watch them suffer from afar.”

At that moment, Beop Gye fell silent.

“Who is committing a crime right now?”

“Y-You bastard...”

“Yes.”

Hae Bang said without any expression.

“The crime of slandering the abbot of the sect isn’t light at all. I will go to the confession hall and reflect on myself.”

With those words, he turned around with no regret, as if he could no longer bear to watch it.

“Y-You fool! Will you not stop right there?”

But Hae Bang didn’t stop walking. Instead, several Shaolin monks, who had been watching Hae Bang, turned their eyes of contempt towards Beop Gye and then followed Hae Bang.

Dozens of Shaolin monks, including Hae Bang, walked away from the Yangtze River, not sparing it a single glance.

“T-those...”

Beop Gye, feeling deeply ashamed, bit his lip.

Even if he disagreed with the abbot, how could one act like this? Was it even possible for a disciple of Shaolin to defy the abbot’s orders and act on their own accord?
