The Black Dragon King saw it clearly. The sword blocking his blade was bent as if it would break at any moment. Blood splashed from Chung Myung’s palm, which pressed against the sword’s body, and his wrist was slowly twisting.

His back bent slightly backward as if it would break, and blood dripped from his mouth.

It all happened in a split second.

But his blade, not yet finished with its attack, kept going. Just by pressing down like this, he would finally kill the kid by splitting him in two!

‘Die!’

It was the very moment when the Black Dragon King was trying to punish Chung Myung’s absurdity by drawing out his internal qi.

Crack!

Chung Myung’s knee, which had converted all flexibility from his body into an offensive attack, mercilessly slammed into the wrist of the Black Dragon King, who was holding down the blade.

“Kuak...”

The first suppressed groan broke through the lips of the Black Dragon King.

At the very moment when the qi he had pulled up was about to flow into the blade, Chung Myung aimed his knee right at the wrist to block the path of the qi flow.

Kwang! Kwang! Kwang!

The knee, which flew in successively, continued to strike the wrist, which was already half split, and the bone exploded.

“Kuak!”

The Black Dragon King finally let out a single scream and flew back.

However, the one he was dealing with was a demon who never knew how to let go of his enemy.

Crack!

Chung Myung’s sword spun around and entangled the retreating Black Dragon King’s blade. Chung Myung, who charged in response, spun around, showing his blood-soaked teeth.

Paaat!

The sword qi, which was shot out from close range, sliced the Black Dragon King’s ankle bone. Before he could groan at the pain that came from his foot, another sword qi flew in from under his body and slashed at his face.

Slash!

Like a sharp blade peeling off the skin of a fruit, the sword qi flew into his face and cut from his chin to his eyes.

In an instant, one side of the Black Dragon King’s vision turned blood red.

“Weikkk!”

The Black Dragon King, who was shouting in a way that was hard to tell whether it was a shout or a scream, swung his blade down with terrifying force.

At that moment, five plum blossoms bloomed right in front of his blade.

Crack!

The fiercely swung blade literally crushed the insignificant flower, but the brief gap created in the process allowed Chung Myung’s body to pass him by.

Slash!

The eerie sensation of the side of his ankle being cut spread through him.

The Black Dragon King somehow managed to keep his body from swaying and turning. At the same time, he spun around and slashed his blade with all his might toward Chung Myung, who was moving away.

“This fucking dogggg!”

KWAAANGGGGG!

The explosive blade qi poured down like a raging storm toward Chung Myung’s back. Anyone familiar with martial arts would be shocked at the sheer power of the attack. But at that moment, Chung Myung turned around and raised his sword.

“AHHHH!”

A powerful downward strike continued from his upper body.

As a clear red line was drawn in the empty space where nothing existed, the blade qi split to the left and right like a thousand-year-old tree being cut down.

Plum blossoms bloomed one after another from the tip of Chung Myung’s sword, which descended downward. They wrapped around the Dark Fragrant Plum Blossom Sword and soon rose up.

At that moment, the Black Dragon King saw it.

The sight of the Plum Blossom qi, which had swelled like a wave above the blade qi, poured down toward him like a waterfall.

Plum Blossom Sword Form, Plum Blossom Flowing Like a River.

Kwaang!

The overflowing enhanced qi covered the blade qi, pushed it, and continued to move, flowing onward.

The river of red petals surged toward the Black Dragon King like a stream swollen by heavy rain.

“BRATTTTT!”

The Black Dragon King bit his lips. A crackling sound echoed in his ears, and blood splashed from his torn lips, but he felt no pain.

His bloodshot eyes drew out all his remaining qi.

Kwang!

He stepped forward and swung the blade, advancing ahead. The river of plum blossoms that poured in crashed into his blade, shattering, crumbling, and bouncing back.

“Woahhhh!”

Kwang!

The Black Dragon King took another step.

Swirling black qi broke through the red qi and advanced toward Chung Myung.

“Woaaah!”

He squeezed out all his strength.

The Black Dragon King, who had spent his entire life in the world of competition, had an intuition at that moment. The one who got pushed there would definitely be the one to die. It was a match where strength met strength, and internal qi clashed with internal qi. It was a spar where he could never afford to be pushed back.

His vision had been hazy for a long time.

The sea of petals around him, the storm of blade qi he had created, had already obscured his sight. Moreover, with one of his eyes cut, he could only see half the vision, unable to perceive beyond the clash of qi.

But he didn’t really need to see. His enemy would be at the end of the river of petals! The moment he took his last step and severed his opponent’s neck that would be his victory!

Puaah!

Blood gushed out from the Black Dragon King’s mouth and nose. The qi he had gathered and pulled up circulated explosively through his body and halted at the tip of his blade.

“AHHHHH!”

The Black Dragon King’s blade accelerated. Faster, stronger! His blade, which had become a storm that would swallow everything, literally slammed down and exploded the pouring plum blossom sword qi.

‘More!’

Just a little more!

His mind gradually emptied. Despite the pain of his dantian being pierced by the sharp blade, the Black Dragon King firmly stepped forward.

And at that moment, the force of the pouring plum blossom sword qi suddenly loosened.

‘Now!’

The Black Dragon King squeezed out all his qi and charged ahead. No, he tried to do so.

Paat!

At that moment, Chung Myung jumped out from the plum blossoms.

It was a surprise that the Black Dragon King would never have missed. But for the first time, he had to fight with just one eye instead of two, and he noticed Chung Myung’s presence a little later than usual.

‘No use though!’

The Black Dragon King swung his blade with all his might.

It didn’t matter if it was a little late! When that guy stabbed the sword into his body, his blade would split the guy’s waist...

But then.

Paaat!

All the muscles in his feet snapped.

The foot, which Chung Myung had cut a moment ago, couldn’t withstand the force of the Black Dragon King.

His body, which had been swinging the qi fiercely, staggered for a moment.

And!

Kwaang!

Chung Myung swung his sword with all his might and struck down the blade of the Black Dragon King. At that moment, the Black Dragon King heard it clearly—the eerie sound of his wrist breaking after enduring so much.

‘You?’

A cut eye.

A severed foot.

A persistently aimed wrist.

Chung Myung, who took advantage of the shock from the clash with the sword, rushed forward instead.

The moment he met those cold, cold eyes, the Black Dragon King realized it.

‘You were aiming for this from the start...?’

Paaat!

The sunset rainbow weapon, which seemed like it would cut down everything in the world, poured down towards the neck of the Black Dragon King.

Slash!

Flutter.

Something that rose into the air spun around violently. Everyone’s eyes on the island were glued to it.

The object, which had spun without pause and splashed blood in all directions, soon lost its strength and crashed.

Thud!

Everyone stared blankly at the sight.

An arm.

A warrior’s arm, trained to the max, crashed into the sand mound. The blood flowing from the cut dyed the white sand red.

No one could even breathe deeply.

It was hard to understand what they were seeing, even with their own eyes. The silence was so thick that even the sound of someone gulping felt like thunder.

Put!

At that moment, the Black Dragon King staggered backward with an eerie sound.

“Ugh....”

The Black Dragon King, who had lost his arm, grabbed his shoulder with his left hand. Blood was spurting out from between his thick fingers.

“Kuak....”

Everyone stared blankly at the sight.

The Black Dragon King’s right arm.

The arm that had commanded the Yangtze River while wielding that giant crescent blade was cut off just below the shoulder. When Chung Myung’s sword moved toward his neck, he reflexively raised his arm to prevent his head from being cut off, but the arm was severed without a trace.

Disbelief, shock. And fear.

With eyes filled with uncontrollable emotions, he stepped back and back.

“T-this...”

Everyone lost their minds as they looked at the Black Dragon King’s condition.

Who would have imagined it?

The sight of the arm of their leader, who reigned like a king on the Yangtze River and was now the leader of the Four Sects Alliance, being cut off by the sword of a third-class disciple of Mount Hua.

However, no matter how many times they closed their eyes and opened them, the sight didn’t change. The moment they realized that all of this was reality, a terrible sense of discomfort began to creep up on everyone on Plum Blossom Island.

Except for one group.

“As expected!”

“Damn it!”

The suppressed exclamations of admiration flowed from the mouths of the Mount Hua disciples.

Their shoulders, which had been released from excessive tension, shook incessantly. They all clenched their fists, forcing themselves to suppress their desire to cheer at any moment.

Baek Cheon also clenched his fists and stared intently at the back of Chung Myung.

“There is no way I wouldn’t believe it.”

“Right.”

“Hmm!”

Jo Gul and Yoon Jong nodded vigorously. Even Yu Yiseol’s shoulders shook slightly.

For the disciples of Mount Hua, who had witnessed the kind of training Chung Myung had endured over the past three years, they couldn’t imagine him losing. If what they had experienced was hell, then what Chung Myung had gone through was literally a hall of infinite suffering.

However, regardless of their beliefs, those wearing Mount Hua’s robes were trembling with endless joy that was now bursting out of their heads.

It felt like they had been rewarded.

Chung Myung clearly proved it to them. The efforts they had made in the past were not in vain. The skills they had accumulated over time were now clearly with their swords.

But, for the swordsmen of the Namgung family who didn’t know Chung Myung so well, this sight was shocking in itself. Their faces were filled with emotions that couldn’t be expressed in words.

“T-that...”

“Oh my...”

They knew. How strong the Black Dragon King was.

The opponent that even Namgung Hwang, the lord of the family, had burned with the fighting spirit was none other than the Black Dragon King. The power of the terrifying blade qi he unleashed was proven by the deaths of those who bore the name of Namgung.

But now, that Black Dragon King was staggering. His arm was cut off by Mount Hua’s Sword Saint.

The result of the fight was all too clear.

“Kuaak...”

The Black Dragon King staggered backward with red eyes. However, the pirates who saw that sight, instead of helping him, retreated in fear.

Wheik.

Chung Myung slightly turned his sword and lowered the tip of his blade.

Kuaak.

Then, he slowly approached the Black Dragon King, scraping the ground with the tip of his sword.

Everyone was in a furor. At the same time, they were shocked.

But Chung Myung’s eyes were not a bit different from the start. He stared at the Black Dragon King with an eerie, sunken gaze and slowly approached him.

“Uh...”

The Black Dragon King’s mouth trembled.

His brain, now emptied, could no longer think. He had no idea what to do from now on. He had never thought that he would be in such a situation.

And then.

Chung Myung did something no one expected.

Kaang!

He kicked the Black Dragon King’s blade, which was on the ground. The blade, which had jumped into the air, landed at the feet of the Black Dragon King.

“...”

A cold voice reached the Black Dragon King’s ears as he watched back and forth between his blade and Chung Myung.

“Grab it.”

Chung Myung continued with a voice devoid of any emotion.

“We need to continue.”

It was a tone that stated the obvious facts.

But the spines of those watching felt a chill run down their backs.

“Your arm is cut?”

“...”

“So what?”

Chung Myung’s eyes were filled with killing intent.

Watching him tremble over the loss of one arm made his stomach turn.

“That moron.”

They didn’t know. Those who lived in peace could not know or understand.

Who protected Kangho? How desperately did they fight? What did it feel like to pull out a sword from the corpse of a comrade who was impaled and swing it?

What were the thoughts of those who fought while trampling on the severed arm with their own feet and biting down on their opponent’s neck?

Those who knew nothing of the situation were acting like the kings of Kangho, behaving arrogantly. Watching such fools made Chung Myung sick.

No, there was no need to dwell on the past.

Chung Myung looked back.

“Namgung...”

At the mention of the Namgung family, the swordsmen of the Namgung family tensed, their muscles clenching.

“Fighting even after their arms were cut off. Until the moment their heads were severed.”

The Namgung swordsmen, overwhelmed with emotion, bowed their heads and bit their lips.

Those words resonated deeply with them, far more than any words of comfort could.

The images of their comrades stabbing their enemies even when their stomachs were slashed open and grabbing their opponent’s ankles even as their heads were being severed remained vivid in their minds. Because of them, those who were here today were alive.

“Catch it.”

“Uh...”

“Catch it, you damn fool.”

The Black Dragon King gritted his teeth and grabbed the crescent blade with his only hand.

And in that moment.

Kwaang!

Chung Myung’s sword suddenly surged forward and struck the crescent blade. The Black Dragon King was helplessly thrown back and fell among the pirates.

As the terrified pirates retreated left and right, the Black Dragon King grabbed one of the pirates who was trying to flee.

“S-stop him.”

“Black Dragon...”

“Stop him! Stop him, you fools! Stop him!”

The Black Dragon King roared in madness. But no one stood in front of Chung Myung for the Black Dragon King’s sake.

It was obvious why.

The reason they followed the Black Dragon King’s orders was out of fear. They were afraid of the retaliation that would come if they disobeyed him.

But wasn’t the end of the Black Dragon King already decided? Why would they be loyal and risk their lives to save him?

The pirates, who had turned pale, were busy stepping back. Even after falling on their asses, they crawled backward. Soon, no one was around the Black Dragon King.

“You damned morons! Didn’t I tell you to stop him? STOP HIMMMM!”

The Black Dragon King shouted as if he were going insane, but his voice was nothing more than empty words.

“Spit!”

Chung Myung, who had spat out the blood that had pooled in his mouth, approached him with indifferent eyes.

The Black Dragon King, who had been glaring at the pirates with a demon-like face, unconsciously stepped back when he saw Chung Myung. The tip of his one remaining finger trembled.

“Uh, uhhhh....”

A groan escaped from his mouth as he forcibly grabbed the crescent blade.

Why did it end up like this?

Before Mount Hua arrived, everything was going his way. Then how on earth did it all end up like this?

His thoughts didn’t last long.

Before he could even get up, Chung Myung ran over and kicked his jaw.

Kwaang!

The Black Dragon King, with his jaw broken, bounced off the ground like a water sparrow and flew away.

Kwaang!

He struck the sand and clutched it in pain.

“Euk...”

He barely turned his head to see Chung Myung approaching him with slow and steady steps.

The Mount Hua swordsmen followed behind him as if they were guarding Chung Myung as he walked among the pirates. The sunlight reflected off their swords, dazzling the eyes.

But... now, there was no one behind the Black Dragon King.

“Uh... uuuhhh....”

No one was helping him.

The pirate troops he had built throughout his life were just watching his approaching death from afar. No one risked their lives for him.

“You ungrateful bastards! Ughhh!”

He let out a furious roar. At that moment, a messenger with a sword arrived in front of him.

“...”

The Black Dragon King blankly looked at Chung Myung.

“I see it.”

“...”

“You aren’t even worth being compared with the feet of Namgung Hwang.”

“Uh...”

“Pirate?”

Chung Myung sneered.

“This is the sandcastle you built, you bastard. You’re weak, and you’re no better than a bug. No one will fight for you.”

“ACKKKK!”

As the Black Dragon King reached out to grab the blade again, Chung Myung’s sword swiftly severed his wrist.

Slash!

“ACKK!”

Before his desperate scream could even end, the sword moved again, slicing through his entire body.

Slash! Slash!

It was as if the Namgung family’s swordsmen intended to leave as many scars on his body as there were on the Namgung swordsmen.

“Eukkk!”

The screams, which made even the listener frown, continued to burst out endlessly.

Under normal circumstances, he wouldn’t have screamed like this, even if his body was crushed. But, since his head had collapsed, he had no strength to resist.

“Shut up!”

However, Chung Myung did not care for the Black Dragon King’s circumstances.

Puak!

The sword pierced the Black Dragon King’s shoulder and twisted. His massive body trembled in pain as all his muscles were cut off.

Pfft!

And the moment his knee was cut, the giant fell forward as if he were collapsing.

Chung Myung stepped on the head and aimed the sword at the throat of the Black Dragon King.

“Insects should die as insects, crawling. Isn’t that right?”

“Uh... uhhh....”

“Go to hell now.”

Chung Myung brought down the sword without any regrets.

But at that moment.

Kwaaang!

With a loud metallic sound, Chung Myung’s sword, which was about to fall on the Black Dragon King’s throat, was pushed aside.

Chung Myung turned his gaze while still stepping on the Black Dragon King’s head. At the end of his cold gaze was a small boat approaching the edge of the island.

To be exact, a man riding on a small boat.

“A bug...”

Jang Ilso, alone on the small ship, smiled as if he found it amusing and looked at Chung Myung.

“Of course, I do not disagree with that... Mount Hua’s Sword Saint.”

His long eyes, drawn with a curved line, stared at Chung Myung.

“Unlike you... there are people in the world who need that bug.”

The small boat carrying Jang Ilso landed on the sand of Plum Blossom Island.

Screech.

The sound of the boat scraping against the sand was unmistakable. After a moment, a man in red slowly disembarked from the halted boat.

“... Jang Ilso.”

Baek Cheon’s face twisted in anger at the sight.

The instant he saw that man’s face, his blood seemed to boil. He longed to rush over and stab the man in the throat with his sword right then. The other disciples standing beside him felt the same, their shoulders trembling.

At that moment, Baek Cheon’s eyes met Chung Myung’s steady gaze. Seeing that silent and motionless figure, Baek Cheon exhaled the breath he had been holding.

From a distance, Jang Ilso, with his smiling face, and Chung Myung, with an expressionless face, locked eyes.

As the two men, whose attire and expressions contrasted starkly, faced each other, everyone on the island held their breath.

Jang Ilso snapped his fingers, and the ring that had fallen to the ground was drawn back into his hand. He slowly placed the ring onto his long, slender finger and glanced at the Black Dragon King.

“... You have turned into a rag.”

His gaze fell on the Black Dragon King’s severed shoulder. Jang Ilso frowned as if he had seen something distasteful. Then, he smiled gently and looked at Chung Myung.

“So, what now, Mount Hua’s Sword Saint? Do you really need his head?”

Chung Myung sneered openly at Jang Ilso’s gentle tone.

“I need it.”

Chung Myung’s foot pressed down on the Black Dragon King.

“Ughh...”

A suppressed groan escaped the Black Dragon King, who couldn’t even resist.

“This?”

Jang Ilso shrugged his shoulders slightly in an exaggerated manner.

“You belong to a sect where talent pours down like rain, so you probably do not understand my feelings, but it isn’t easy to find someone like him in the Evil Sect.”

“...”

“Whether it’s a bug or a crippled person, I have to pick up and use whatever I can. Isn’t that a pitiful thing?”

“You seem to have your mouth running as ever.”

“I’m glad you acknowledge that. It is your specialty.”

Jang Ilso chuckled.

“So... how about you endure killing him?”

“...”

“The Black Dragon King, who lost his arm and is now a crippled person, wouldn’t be a threat to you. So if you just leave him here, I will pick him up and use him.”

“Why should I do that?”

Chung Myung’s cold gaze seemed ready to pierce Jang Ilso at any moment. He glared at him for a moment before chuckling.

“No, no, I can do it.”

He glared at Jang Ilso, baring his teeth like a wolf under attack, filling it with all the hostility he could muster.

“If you are willing to give up even that worthless head as payment, then I can.”

“Hahahaha.”

Jang Ilso slowly stroked his white throat.

“Oh my, I didn’t know this head was so valuable. It’s something even Mount Hua’s Sword Saint wants to covet.”

His pale eyes were clearly filled with madness. Just looking at those eyes would be enough to scare a person with little courage.

“But that is difficult. No matter how insignificant a head is, it is still precious to trade life with a bug.”

The Black Dragon King trembled under Chung Myung’s feet. Even though he was barely breathing, thanks to Jang Ilso, this kind of treatment was too cruel for him, who was the absolute ruler of the Yangtze River.

But he couldn’t even open his mouth.

The moment he let out his anger, Chung Myung’s sword would cut off his head without hesitation. Having faced Chung Myung himself, the Black Dragon King knew. This guy wasn’t a man who hesitated to kill.

“Then tell.”

Chung Myung spoke in a toneless voice.

“Tell me with that smart mouth of yours. Why should I let this guy live? And why should I...”

His gaze pierced right through Jang Ilso.

“Be sparing you?”

Jang Ilso’s red lips curved into a clear, unsettling smile.

‘Creepy.’

This was no joke. Whenever he met those eyes, a chill always ran down his spine.

‘I thought you would return as a tiger.’

This was more of a monster than a tiger.

Jang Ilso’s pure white hands, hanging down the sleeves, tightened, and the rings rubbed against each other, making a rattling sound.

Jang Ilso glanced down at his hands. His eyes darkened for a moment.

When one adorned the entire body with jewelry, one could gauge one’s condition most accurately. The fact that his hand was clenched involuntarily meant he was currently feeling an extreme killing intent towards Chung Myung.

‘Do I want to kill him?’

Right here? In such a place?

Was it worth throwing away everything he had planned so far just to kill him? Was Mount Hua’s Sword Saint such a threatening person?

His mind hadn’t finished assessing Mount Hua’s Sword Saint yet, but his instincts were clearly telling him so.

He had to kill Mount Hua’s Sword Saint right there, at that moment, at any cost.

Instincts control the will to live. Right then, his instincts saw that little swordsman as a clearer threat than anyone Jang Ilso had ever met.

Jang Ilso laughed softly and covered his face with his hands.

He didn’t want to show anyone the killing intent that might be on his face. He covered his face, but the cold light flowed through his eyes, revealed between his long fingers.

I want to cut him down.

I want to cut off that head and soak my hands in his blood,

But...

“The price...”

Of course, Jang Ilso rejected the voice of his heart.

Unable to control his impulses, unable to distinguish between right and wrong, and acting only according to his instincts. That is what the pigs he detested the most did.

“What do you want, Mount Hua’s Sword Saint?”

“...”

“Tell me what you want. I will give you anything that I can. Even if it’s...”

A smooth, poisonous voice lingered in Chung Myung’s ears.

“Even if you don’t have to bow your heads to the floor and beg for your life, it is an infinite mercy of mine ready to send you off this island.”

“This...”

The moment those words were spoken, all of Mount Hua’s disciples began to emit a blue-green killing intent. Their eyes were red, and they had extended their legs, holding their swords as if they were ready to rush at Jang Ilso at any moment.

“Do not act rashly.”

The one who suppressed the atmosphere, which seemed like it would explode at any moment, was Un Geom. As soon as he finished speaking, the anger that had been sweeping through the Mount Hua disciples vanished.

It couldn’t be anything else.

Un Geom’s words were always heavy, but they were even more so in front of the Ten Thousand People Clan and Jang Ilso. No one could be more angry in front of Un Geom.

The swordsmen of Mount Hua, who had regained their composure, shut their mouths tightly. But their swords were still aimed at Jang Ilso, emitting a bright blue light.

At that moment, Chung Myung spoke again.

“Jang Ilso.”

“Hmm?”

Chung Myung’s eyes held no emotion.

“Do not mistake the situation.”

Jang Ilso looked at him with a deep smile.

“Mistake? Me?”

“You have no idea.”

At that moment, Jang Ilso’s face changed slightly. Chung Myung’s words sounded meaningful, not like empty talk.

Chung Myung continued.

“Why do you think you didn’t lose your head even after setting foot on this island?”

Jang Ilso looked down at Chung Myung and stayed silent.

“Did you think I couldn’t kill you? Did you think that I would accept your offer to save one more person?”

Chung Myung laughed, showing his teeth.

“You stupid, Evil Sect bastard, you think the world only goes around the way you want it to.”

Jang Ilso smiled brightly at the harsh words and stroked his chin.

“Then what is the reason?”

“....”

“May I ask what the great intention of Mount Hua’s Sword Saint, who protects the harmony of the Central Plains, is to spare this vicious Evil Sect leader?”

Despite the provocation delivered with laughter, Chung Myung didn’t react at all. He simply spoke.

“It is tiring to tell an idiot such an answer, but a Taoist also must guide you. Wash those ears and listen carefully, you moron. There is only one reason why I won’t kill you.”

“....”

“Because it isn’t my decision.”

Jang Ilso frowned.

It was a very small change in his expression, but those who had seen Jang Ilso up until then could clearly see it. It was the first time a suspicious expression had appeared on his face, which always looked too confident.

“... What are you talking about?”

“Even a three-year-old would know what it means.”

Chung Myung coldly retorted.

“I should explain it to a fool like you, after all. I am just a sword of Mount Hua. There is only one person in the world who decides who Mount Hua’s Sword will be aimed at.”

“....”

At that moment, Jang Ilso’s gaze moved. Beyond Chung Myung, to the person behind him.

“If you want to save your pitiful life and go back, bow your head and pray to the sect leader. If not him, no one here will be able to save you.”

Clench.

The Dark Fragrant Plum Blossom Sword next to the Black Dragon King’s throat scratched the sand. It was as if the sect leader had ordered the sword to cut off the head of Jang Ilso right then.

A faint sense of bewilderment crossed Jang Ilso’s face, which had always been smooth as if he was drawing.

‘You are moving according to the orders of the sect leader?’

Did it even make sense?

Of course, he belonged to an Evil Sect, and they were a Justice Sect. Unlike the Evil Sect, where power dictated everything, he knows that Justice has its own set of rules that must be followed. Hadn’t Jang Ilso used that fact to his advantage?

But that was just the story of ordinary people.

There was no way someone as extraordinary as Mount Hua’s Sword Saint could be controlled by that dull and lacking sect leader. Even when spoken objectively, wasn’t Hyun Jong not even in the category of ordinary?

And yet, Mount Hua’s Sword Saint, who was arrogant enough to look down on the Nine Great Sects and the leaders of the Five Great Families that ruled this world, held such vicious intent and displayed killing intent in the face of Jang Ilso, the leader of the Four Precepts Alliance, following the orders of Hyun Jong?

Jang Ilso was so shocked that he almost chuckled. But the strange statement was soon proven to be true.

Despite Chung Myung’s ridiculous words, no one in Mount Hua wavered their eyes. Not even a hint of suspicion crossed their minds.

That meant they were taking Chung Myung’s words seriously. It wasn’t just a simple improvisation, but that Mount Hua’s Sword Saint had held those words all along.

The moment he realized that Jang Ilso broke into a cold sweat. It had been years since he felt so flustered and nervous.

‘Then I guess I did enter the cave on my own feet.’

He thought the subject of the negotiation would naturally be Mount Hua’s Sword Saint. That was why he had come there alone. Mount Hua’s Sword Saint, with his extraordinary intelligence, would not be able to ignore the fact that Jang Ilso died; there would be a loss for Mount Hua.

But... what if the one to negotiate was Hyun Jong?

Jang Ilso twisted his lips and burst into laughter.

‘Maybe this could be my grave.’

He slowly straightened his back and walked confidently towards Hyun Jong, who was watching him.

Flutter.

The hem of the long robe flapped wildly. Jang Ilso, having slightly adjusted his attire, bowed deeply toward Hyun Jong, executing a perfect bow without any flaws.

“Jang Ilso of the Four Precepts Alliance.”

Jang Ilso looked at Hyun Jong with eyes like the waning moon.

They had already met once before, yet he was as polite as if he were meeting Hyun Jong for the first time.

“I greet you, the leader of the Heavenly Friends Alliance and sect leader of Mount Hua.”

Hyun Jong let out a low sigh and bowed to Jang Ilso, who greeted him.

“I am Hyun Jong, the Alliance leader of the Heavenly Friends Alliance and sect leader of Mount Hua. Nice to meet you, Hegemony Lord.”

As expected, the man was extremely polite.

However, the coldness of the blade hidden within swept through the hearts of all who watched the scene.
